門間 一夫の 
経済深読み

成長戦略より21 世紀ニッポンの「 ア
ポロ計画」を 
みずほ総合研究所  
エグゼクティブエコノミスト  門間一夫 202 6 年 10 月 27 日

心情的にはノーサレンダー路線 
先月の論考では、①度重なる成長戦略にもかかわらず日本の潜在成長率は低下したこと、②成長戦略
には政策手段の特定が困難という基本問題があるこ と 、③25 年英知を尽くしても結果が出なかった事実
は厳粛に受け止めるべきこと、などを述べた 1。 
それを踏まえ、本稿ではあるべき方向性を考えてみたい。選択肢は、(a)25 年で諦めず50 年でも100
年でもやる、(b)成長目標を達成しやすい水準に下げる、(c)政府のミッションは成長戦略とは別にある
と考える、の 3 つである。選択肢(b)が無難に見えるが、もともと成長戦略は「現状の成長率ではだめ
だ」という認識に基づくものである。現状を明確に上回る成長率を目指さない限り、成長戦略としては
自己矛盾である。 
すると残るのは (a)ノーサレンダー路線、(c)ミッション再考、 のいずれかである。このうちノーサレ
ンダー路線、つまり「成長を諦めない」という不屈の姿勢には人々の共感が得られやすい。しかし、不
屈の志を掲げても、手段の裏付けを欠くという現実は変わらない。手段の裏付けを欠くなら「目標」で
はなく「夢」である。もちろん、夢を語ることが悪いわけではないし、政治家は夢を語るべきという考
えもあるだろう。その夢が偶然にでもかなえば歴史に残る偉業であり、夢なら実現せずとも落胆の度合
いは小さい。ノーサレンダー路線はアップサイドだけでダウンサイドはない、と考えてよいだろうか。 
成長戦略のダウンサイド 
成長戦略にダウンサイドはない、やってみて損はない、と い う前提は実は正しくない。成長戦略は「た
だ」でできるわけではないからだ。例えば、成長戦略には多かれ少なかれネット財政支出が伴う。国債
をどれだけ発行できるのか議論の余地はあるが、いずれにせよ有限である。 
一方、成長戦略は総花的になりやすい。成長を実現する手段がはっきりしない以上、ある政策案を「成
長とは無関係」ときっぱり排除するのも難しいからである。経済界の要望や政治家の信念に基づく様々
な政策案が、成長に少しでも関係しそうなら幅広く正当化される。各種施策の費用対効果について一定

1 門間一夫の経済深読み「成長戦略の本質的な限界」 （2026年 9 月25 日 ）。

### Page 2

2

の分析や議論は行われている。しかし、行われることとそれが有効に機能することは別である。費用対
効果の分析が有効に機能し、各種施策の経済成長へのインパクトが正確に測れていたとしたら、25 年に
わたり成長目標の大幅未達という結果にはならなかったはずである。 
一般に、政策目標の実現に向けてリソースが投入され、目標が実現できなかった場合、そのリソース
投入は失敗だったことになる。チャレンジ自体は無駄ではなかったと言える場合もあるが、過去 25 年
だめだった目標にもう一度挑戦してみるという話なら、たぶん次もだめだろうと事前に予想できる。金
も人もふんだんにあるなら別だが、財政状況が厳しく、人材の不足も叫ばれる国で、高確率で失敗する
ミッションへのリソース投入は賢明な選択と言えるのか。 
有限のリソースが無駄に使われれば、他の課題のために使えるリソースが減る。ここで言うリソース
は財政資金だけではない。実現できない目的のために、多忙な官僚の労力が割かれ、政治家のポリティ
カル ・ キャピタルも消耗すれば、やればできたはずの他の課題への対応まで中途半端に終わってしまう。
成長戦略には他の課題を犠牲にするという「機会費用」が伴うのであり、それが「ただではない」とい
うことのより正確な意味である。「成長を諦めないノーサレンダー路線でいく」という選択は、「50 年で
も 100 年でもその機会費用を払い続けることを覚悟する」という選択でもある。 
何のための経済成長か 
ここで話を一段階前に戻してみよう。そもそもなぜ、経済成長を高めるべきと考えられているのだろ
うか。もちろん、低成長より高成長の方がいいに決まっているので、経済成長を簡単に高められるので
あれば、そんな疑問を持つ必要はない。しかし、経済成長を高めようとすることで機会費用を払い続け
ることになる確率が高いなら、そうまでしてわれわれが最終的に望んでいるものは何なのかという問い
に、きちんと向き合う必要がある。 
経済成長の必要性を訴える主張は、「〇〇の解決には経済成長が不可欠」「低成長のままなら〇〇のよ
うな不都合が起きる」というパターンにおおむね当てはまるように思う。つまり、現在直面している課
題、あるいは将来直面しそうな課題に対応するには、より高い経済成長が必要という認識が成長戦略の
基礎にあると考えられる。念頭に置かれている課題の中身は、社会保障や財政の持続性、行政・医療・
介護サービスの維持、国際的な存在感の回復、生活水準の向上など、論者により様々である。パイを増
やせるなら改善できる課題は多いので、経済成長を高めるべきという主張は万人受けし、政治家には格
好の政策アジェンダになる。 
しかし、「何らかの課題を経済成長で解決する」という考え方は 、「その課題に直接向き合うより経済
成長を高める方が簡単だ」という前提がないと成立しない。その前提が間違っていれば、むしろ経済成
長に頼るがゆえに肝心の課題がいつまでも解決しないことになる。重要な課題であればあるほど、経済
成長と切り離して解決する努力を早く始めるべきではないか。 
例えば、社会保障や財政の持続性が課題なのであれば、給付と負担のバランスや、恵まれている人と
そうでない人の公平感を改善することで、問題の解決を目指す方が良い。人々の生活水準を高めたいと
いう課題を経済成長と切り離すのは少し厄介だが、国民全員が等しく困っているわけではなく、問題の

### Page 3

3

本質は経済格差にある。分配政策により、国民生活の満足感や社会の安定性を全体として改善すること
はできる。 
国際社会における日本の存在感を高めることは、経済成長ではそもそも無理であろう。かつて日本が
世界第2 位のGDP をキープし、経済大国として存在感を示した時代もあった。しかし、あれは冷戦構造
のもとでの日米関係、戦後の高度成長とその余熱、新興国が発展初期段階にあったことなどによる歴史
の偶然であった。GDP の対世界シェアが3～4％程度まで落ち、今後さらに人口が減り、資源・エネルギ
ーに乏しく、特殊な言語を使う島国が、今から経済成長を多少高められたとしても、そのことで示せる
国際パワーは高が知れている。小さくても存在感がある国をどう作るかが、正しいアジェンダ設定だと
思われる。 
21 世紀ニッポンの「アポロ計画」は？ 
日本が課題だらけなのは確かだが、25 年間どうやっても達成できなかった経済成長に、その解決をこ
れ以上頼るのが正解とは思いにくい。逆に、優先課題の解決に国が本気でリソースを投入するなら、そ
こに民間も収益機会を見出し、様々な連鎖反応を通じて経済に好影響が及ぶ可能性も出てくる。 
経済学者マリアナ・マッツカートは著書「ミッション・エコノミー」において、大きな変革は市場メ
カニズムだけでは生み出せず、国家が野心的だが実行可能な課題を設定し、民間の力を引き出す仕組み
を築くことが重要と説く 2。マッツカートは代表例として、1960 年代の米国アポロ計画を挙げる。1969
年 7 月 20 日、 ニール・アームストロングとバズ・オルドリンが月面に降り立ったことで知られる、あの
アポロ計画である。 
日本の成長戦略も「野心的な課題」を掲げたものではあるが、複雑すぎるマクロ経済そのものを課題
の対象にすると、先月の論考で詳しく述べたように、目的と手段の関係性が特定できない。原因と結果
の関係がよくわからない仕事は、責任の所在があいまいで互いに人任せになり、結果にコミットするこ
との効果が得られない。それに対し、「月に人を送る」などのより具体的なミッションの場合、そのため
には何が必要なのかの因果関係を分析・認識しやすい分、結果にコミットすれば人々の力が正しい方向
へ向かう。 
アポロ計画は、半導体、コンピューター、通信、材料工学などの技術を飛躍的に発展させ、その後の
米国の産業基盤を築いたと言われている。また、計画推進の国家機関であった米航空宇宙局 （NASA）は 、
多くの科学技術人材を育成・輩出した。彼らはその後、金融イノベーションなどでも活躍し、NASA 出身
なので「ロケット・サイエンティスト」と呼ばれたことはよく知られている。 
ここで重要なのは、半導体やコンピューターなどの産業育成自体を目的として、政府が補助金で支援
したという話ではないことである。アポロ計画という具体的なミッションがまず存在し、それへの貢献
に適正な対価で報いる仕組みがイノベーターたちを突き動かし、様々な技術や知見が開発、蓄積された
のである。後世に残った産業基盤や人材は、「月に人を送る」というミッションの副産物であった。

2 Mazzucato, Mariana(2021) Mission Economy: A Moonshot Guide to Changing Capitalism, Allen Lane-Penguin 
（関美和・鈴木絵里子訳『ミッション・エコノミー』News Picks、2021 年 ）。

### Page 4

4

一般論として、何らかの課題を解決すれば、経済に必ず大きなプラスの効果が見込める、とまでは言
えない。しかし、上位目標が「課題の解決」なのだから、それが達成された時点でミッションは成功で
あり、投入されたリソースは「有効に使われた」ことになる。そのうえで、マクロ経済にもプラスの波
及効果があればなお良いが、仮にそれがなくても課題が解決された分、以前より望ましい社会になって
いる。 
最大の難関は、優先課題を特定しそれに取り組む社会的モメンタムを生み出すことであり、それには
既存の発想に捉われない強力なビジョンとリーダーシップが要る。アポロ計画にも当初「無謀で財政資
金の無駄使い」という声があった。マッツカートは先ほどの著書の中で、仮に現代風の費用対効果分析
を適用していたら、アポロ計画は採用されず、人類が月面に立つことはなかったと言っている。 
アポロ計画に相当する案件は、どの時代、どの国にも自明なものとしてあるわけではない。 「月に人を
送る」というミッションは、当時の米ソ冷戦状況において、ソ連に宇宙開発で打ち勝つことが米国の死
活問題、という歴史特殊的な認識のもとで生まれた。それでも、よく考えてみれば今の日本にも、人口
の減少 ・ 高齢化や地政学環境の激変など、 人類史上にもまれな歴史特殊的な条件のもとで、 「死活問題」
になりつつある課題はいくつかあるように思う。例えば、エネルギーや食料の安定供給、生活インフラ ・
医療・介護体制の維持・改善などは、次世代の日本人にとってまさに「死活問題」なのではないか。 
限られたリソースの優先課題への配分が、市場メカニズムで実現できないなら、国が強力に関与しな
ければならない。民間には不得意な国民的課題の解決こそ、国の本来の役割であり、そのためのリソー
スを国債発行でまかないきれないなら、国民に追加負担を求めてもいい。 
言うまでもなくこれは困難な作業である。だから優先課題は何か、そのために国民負担を求めるべき
かなどを深く議論せず、経済成長で様々な問題をまとめて解決できることにしておく方が、実はずっと
楽なのである。しかし、それが問題の先送りにすぎないことは繰り返し述べたとおりである。今も成長
戦略が重要だと言い、政府にそれを求めたがるわれわれの意識は、日本が直面する真の課題やその優先
順位を考えるのは面倒だ、 という思考停止から生まれているのかもしれない。21 世紀ニッポンの「アポ
ロ計画」は何なのか、国民的な議論の活性化が必要ではないだろうか。

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
 隨著法國距離下屆總統大選僅剩六個月，極左翼與政府間的政治對立升溫，然而
法國政府急需推動預算案以控制財政赤字與龐大債務。在內政危機加劇與工會
預告 11月大罷工的背景下，市場對法國債務可持續性的擔憂升溫，將帶動法德
公債利差維持高檔，進而推升歐元區主權風險溢酬並拖累歐元表現。
 上週路透最新調查顯示，在 100 位受訪經濟學家中，高達 94%預期聯準會將於
10 月會議維持利率在 3.75%-4.00% 不變，惟全員一致預估 12 月將重啟升息。
儘管美國受 AI 投資熱潮帶動、經濟成長強勁，且美伊戰事推升油價突破百元
大關、加徵關稅使 PCE 通膨至少在 2028 年前難以回到 2% 目標，官員仍傾
向審慎行事。不過逾四成專家警告，聯準會恐落後通膨形勢，後續面臨至少再升
息兩次甚至擴大升息幅度的上行風險，預期美元大幅回檔的空間小，逢低承接買
盤依然強勁。
 路透最新調查顯示，受日圓疲軟與中東戰事推升能源及進口成本、加劇通膨壓力

影響，近七成受訪經濟學家預計日本央行將於 12月再度升息至 1.50%，延續 6
月與 9 月以來的較快緊縮步伐；同時，逾半數專家預估終端利率將上看 2.00%
或更高。不過，高達近四分之三受訪者警告，若高市早苗內閣提名的鴿派委員再
次投下反對票，恐壓抑日圓表現。在歐美央行年底前亦可能升息的背景下，日本
央行維持較快的緊縮節奏，有助於防止美日、歐日利差進一步擴大，避免日圓在
年底遭遇新一輪劇烈拋售，為日圓提供實質的下檔保護。

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Oct/27/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週台幣匯價高點為 32.360，低點在 32.704，以 32.508作收，較前一周收盤價
貶值 1 角 7 分。 
上週台幣走貶，周初，外資延續前周大幅匯出的走勢，台幣跳空開低，跌破今
年 7 月的低點 32.528；隨著台股表現不佳，外資賣超台股並匯出，台幣續創低位，
最低來到 32.704周內低點；而後台股低檔反彈，外資由匯出轉為雙向進出，加以出
口商積極進場拋售美金，台幣由貶轉升至 32.500下方；臨近周末，市場擔憂颱風來
襲周五可能休市，增添連續假期變數，外資再度大幅賣超台股並匯出，令台幣又跌
回 32.600上方，終場接在央行調節之下收在 32.508。 
＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週台幣走勢偏弱。近期全球科技股因 AI 類股估值偏高及融資風險疑慮
而持續修正，市場風險偏好降溫，股市面臨修正，外資連續賣超台股並匯出，台幣
走勢持續面臨壓力。另一方面，美伊戰事和談進展的消息反覆，令國際油價節節攀
升，突破每桶 110美元上方，引發通膨升溫的擔憂，支撐利率維持在高檔的預期，
美元指數也在 101.5 上方高位震盪，將不利台幣的表現。盤勢上來看，外資隨著股
市回檔修正表現偏向賣超台股並匯出，台幣偏弱，不過本周適逢月底，預計匯價逢
高仍持續有出口商拋售美金的需求，將限制台幣走貶的幅度。綜合以上，預估本週
台幣走勢偏弱。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：32.300-32.800

美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Oct/27/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣走升。周初中國公布9月工業生產物價指數 5.5%，表現優於預期的
5.4%(前值 5.2%)，且第三季 GDP表現 4.5%，也優於預期的 4.4%(前值 4.3%)，基本
面穩健支撐人民幣升破 6.6800 整數關口；不過周間美元指數持續於 101.5 上方高位
震盪，加以美伊戰事未見緩解，國際油價持續飆升，突破每桶 110 美元上方，人民
幣於 6.6800下方高檔震盪；臨近周末，人行中間價格連續走升，升破6.7200整數關
口，週五最高來到 6.7180，顯示中國人行允許人民幣溫和升值的走勢，終場境外人
民幣升破 6.6700整數關口 ， 收在6.6690， 週線升值160點 ， 境內人民幣則收在6.6704，
週線升值 166 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣偏升。就中國國內市場來看，人行中間價持續偏升，將持續帶
動人民幣走升，加以出口商實質拋匯需求將支撐人民幣表現；就國際市場來看，最
新利率期貨顯示 ， 本周聯準會利率決議有9成的機率維持利率在 3.75%~4.00%不變 ，
不過年底前仍有一碼的升息空間，加以地緣政治風險未見緩解，能源價格高檔震盪
推升通膨與利率預期，美元指數維持在 101.5 上方高檔震盪，將限制人民幣升值的
速度跟幅度；最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH日線圖中，各天期均線向下發散，匯價
維持在五日均線之下交投，均線呈現空頭排列，顯示空方勢力較盛，綜合以上，預
估本週人民幣偏升。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6300-6.6900

美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Oct/27/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週 USD/JPY 震盪下跌。週初，USD/JPY 大致在 158 的整數關卡上下波動；
週間，AI相關概念股遭拋售，加之中東局勢緊張推升避險需求，10年期美債殖利率
跌破 5%，避險資金亦流入日圓，帶動 USD/JPY 下行最低來至 156.67；臨近週末，
市場避險需求持續加之日本 9 月通膨數據高於日本央行 2%目標，USD/JPY 一度下
跌至 156.06，後逢低買盤進場跌幅有所收斂，終場收在 156.86，週線下跌 113點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY區間震盪，主要原因如下： 一、美國10 月 S&P製造業 PMI
為 56.0高於 9 月的 55.9且為 53個月高點，服務業則為 57.9低於 9月的 58.8，綜合
PMI為 57.6亦低於 9 月的 58.4，顯示美國商業活動雖略有放緩但仍處於擴張，經濟
基本面依然穩健將持續支撐美元；此外，中東局勢惡化使能源供應風險上升，布蘭
特原油價格突破 116美元，為六個月以來高點，也將有利USD/JPY上行。二、日本
9 月全國整體消費者物價指數（CPI）年增 2.1%，且 9 月核心 CPI年增 2.1%，為九
個月以來首次超過日本央行 2%的目標，強化市場對日本央行短期升息的預期，預計
為日圓帶來支撐。三、就技術面而言，日線圖中，USD/JPY匯價處於各天期均線之
下且接連跌破 158 及 157 關卡，後在 156 整數關卡暫時獲得支撐，顯示逢低仍有買
盤進場，將限縮下行幅度。綜合以上，預估USD/JPY本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較
高。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日幣：155.00-158.50

美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
         Oct/27/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週 EUR/USD震盪下跌。週初，EUR/USD在 1.1200整數關卡窄幅波動；週間
受到油價及美債殖利率回落，外加法國社會局勢略見緩解影響，帶動匯價反彈至
1.1260遇到賣壓後回落，後續大致在1.1190至 1.1250間波動；臨近週末，歐元區10
月份 PMI表現低迷，且市場對於法國政局和財政赤字擔憂持續，拖累 EUR/USD轉
若，匯價一度下行來至 1.1183，終場收在 1.1202，週線下跌 37點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD震盪偏空，主要原因如下：一、上週公佈歐元區10月 PMI
顯示德、法表現分化，法國製造業及服務業均表現疲弱低於50榮枯線，拖累歐元區
10 月綜合 PMI 由 9 月 53.1 下滑至 51.9，為四個月以來新低，經濟基本面承壓將不
利 EUR/USD上行；此外，法國社會及政經局勢不穩導致法債殖利率突破5%，市場
對其財政及政治危機擔憂持續，也將不利歐元表現。二、美國上週初請失業金人數
降至 19.4萬人，為三個月以來新低，顯示勞動力市場依然穩健，且10月 S&P製造
業 PMI為 56.0續創 53個月新高，服務業及綜合PMI維持穩健，顯示美國經濟仍具
韌性，預期將限縮 EUR/USD上行空間。三、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD
各天期均線維持空頭排列，且匯價持續承壓於月線之下，顯示短線空方趨勢仍未改
變，在能有效突破上方月線前仍以震盪偏空看待。綜合以上，預估 EUR/USD 本週
呈現震盪偏空的可能性較高。

新台幣利率走勢分析

115.10.19-115.10.22  
銀行隔夜拆款利率 0.805% ~ 0.825%  
30天期短票利率   1.67% ~ 1.68%  
  公債－10年期- A15109 2.000%~2.002% 
      － 5 年期- A15108     1.88%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分，儘管市場預期強烈颱風「薔琵」來襲，週五金融市場可能
因颱風假而暫停交易，銀行間多預留帳上資金以應不時之需，但市況仍屬充
裕，即便有新增調度需求，也很容易滿足，令短率朝區間低位移動。 
週四隔夜拆款利率區間在 0.805%~0.825%， 30 天期短票主要成交在
1.65%~1.68%，利率低位較上週下跌約 1 個基本點。

二、 本週市場交投冷清，10 年期指標券 A15109 週四於等殖系統成交在 2.002%，
5 年期指標券 A15108則未成交，最近成交利率為 1.88%。 
週二 10 年期公債標售得標利率 2.001%，落於市場預期區間下緣，並創 2008
年以來新高，顯示市場持續反映經濟成長與通膨預期。 
考量通膨前景及國際債市走勢仍具不確定性，市場觀望氣氛濃厚；惟第四季公
債供給相對有限，可望抑制殖利率大幅彈升。預期短線台債殖利率維持區間整
理，5 年券及 10 年券交易區間分別估於 1.82%~1.84%及 1.98%~2.01%。

Stronger revenue momentum while GM guidance came in softer 
TSMC hosted its 3Q26 analyst meeting on October 15. While we 
view the higher full-year 2026 revenue guidance and the further 
acceleration in capacity buildout as the key positives, the GM 
guidance could be the softer spot. In the meeting, management 
further raised its 2026 revenue growth outlook to grow by around 
44% YoY (from slightly above 40%; USD term), supported by 
continued strong demand especially for AI/HPC. However, both 
3Q26 GM result and 4Q26 GM guidance came in lower than our 
expectations; we believe this is to reflect a steeper N2 ramp and 
overseas fab dilution in the near term. 
AI demand still ahead of supply, with no sign of slowdown 
Key questions among investors largely surrounded demand outlook. 
Overall, we think that management’s tone on AI demand remained 
very bullish. While TSMC did not formally update its long-term AI 
accelerator revenue CAGR, management indicated the CAGR will 
probably be higher, noting demand is extremely robust with no sign 
of slowdown and still far exceeds supply, with multiple drivers 
reinforcing this view, including - 1) agentic AI driving incremental 
CPU demand, which is excluded from TSMC’s AI accelerator 
definition and implies upside beyond the headline CAGR; 2) broader 
adoption of open-source models lowering the barrier to AI usage 
and lifting leading-edge silicon demand; 3) continued demand for 
networking chips alongside AI accelerators; and 4) TSMC’s own 
checks on US data center build progress and power supply, where 
management sees CSPs as very well prepared.  
Capacity build-out accelerating again: N2 capacity more than 
double in 2027; additional N3 fab addition; Texas under evaluation 
On N2, management for the first time guided its N2 capacity will be

comes on top of 1) the ongoing N3 fab expansion in Tainan (ramp in 1H27), 2) Arizona
fab 2 (ramp in 3Q27, slightly ahead of its prior schedule), 3) Japan fab 2 (ramp in 2028),
and 4) continued N5-to-N3 tool conversion. Beyond Arizona, TSMC confirmed it is
evaluating Texas as a potential additional US site, and is in discussion with one customer

building Terafab on utilizing that capacity; while the business model and structure
remains under discussion, TSMC highlighted that its principle is to run the fab by itself.
GM: steeper N2 dilution near term, but “no ceiling” long term
3Q26 GM declined 1.3ppt QoQ to 66.4%, primarily on dilution from the initial N2 ramp,
and 4Q26 GM is guided down a further 40bps to 66.0% at the mid-point on continued
N2 dilution and overseas fabs. Management now expects overseas fab dilution to be

closer to 2% for full-year 2026 (vs. 2-3% previously), helped by larger scale and cost
improvement, but reiterated it will widen to 3-4% in the latter part of the five-year
period as more US fabs ramp. Longer term, management stated there is “no ceiling” for
TSMC’s profitability, though any dramatic increase will not happen overnight. On pricing,
management reiterated a strategic approach by segment, location and technology, with
US production priced differently from Taiwan. Overall, we slightly lower our GM to

66.6%/66.8%/67.7% in 2026-2028E (vs. 67.2%/67.5%/67.8% previously).
CapEx: 2026 unchanged, multi-year step-up reiterated
2026 CapEx guidance was unchanged at US$60-64bn. While management did not
provide numeric guidance beyond 2026, but reiterated that 2026-2028 CapEx will be
significantly higher than the prior three years, and that revenue growth should continue
to outpace CapEx growth. Management acknowledged the tools, material and labor
inflation, but said the majority of CapEx is driven by long-term demand. Net net, we

maintain our 2026E/2027E/2028E CapEx at US$64bn/US$85bn/US$98bn.
High confidence in its structural lead
Addressing questions regarding rising competition, management remains confident in its
technology lead  for both front end wafer and advanced packaging technology. Despite
the later adoption timeframe (planned for in 2030) vs peers on High NA EUV, TSMC does
not see the later adoption than peers as a disadvantage. In advanced packaging, TSMC is
in volume production at 5x reticle size, which management believes no peer can match

today, with 9x and 14x in development and CoPoS for future cost reduction. In addition,
management also highlighted that it expects A14 (volume production in 2028) to be a
bigger node than N2, with customer engagement already exceeding N2’s at the same
stage.

### Page 5

3Q26 results and 4Q26 guidance recap

4Q26 guidance: revenue well above expectations, GM below GSe 
For 4Q26, management guided revenue to US$52.3-54.3bn (+14% QoQ at the 
mid-point; based on FX [TWD/USD] of 31.8), GM to 65-67% and OpM to 56.5-58.5%. 
Revenue guidance came in well above GSe of +11.0% QoQ, supported by continued 
strong leading-edge demand including the steep N2 ramp. However, the GM mid-point 
of 66.0% is 1.3ppt below GSe of 67.3% and broadly in line with consensus of 66.1%, as 
continued N2 dilution and overseas fabs are only partly offset by productivity gains and 
cross-node capacity optimization. 
3Q26 results: revenue and EPS beat, GM below GSe 
3Q26 revenue of NT$1,494.2bn (US$46.72bn) was up 17.6% QoQ and 50.9% YoY, above 
the high end of guidance in USD terms and above GSe/consensus by 0.8%/2.1%. EPS of 
NT$29.43 was up 8.0% QoQ and 68.7% YoY, above GSe/consensus by 1.2%/3.7%; 
excluding the NT$2.24 one-off gain from the VIS disposal in 2Q26, EPS grew 18% QoQ. 
GM of 66.4% (-1.3ppt QoQ) landed in the upper half of guidance (65-67%) but 1.1ppt 
below GSe, while OpM of 58.7% beat the high end of guidance (56-58%) on stable OpEx 
at 7.8% of revenue. 
By technology, N2 contributed 8% of wafer revenue (vs. 3% in 2Q26) and N3 33% (vs. 
30%), taking advanced nodes (7nm and below) to 79% (vs. 77%). By platform, HPC rose 
22% QoQ to 68% of revenue (vs. 66%), with smartphone +10% QoQ at 21%. Inventory 
days fell 5 days QoQ to 82 days on higher wafer shipments.

### Page 7

Reiterate Buy; 12m TP maintain at NT$3,300 
We maintain our 12m TP at NT$3,300  based on a target P/E of 22x (unchanged, 
benchmarked to 1.0stdv above 5-year avg. forward P/E) applied to our 2027E EPS. 
Accordingly, we maintain our 12 month TP for the ADR at US$660, based on an 
unchanged FX conversion of USD/TWD of 30.0 and unchanged ADR premium of 20%. 
Our 22x target P/E multiple is also benchmarked to TSMC’s trading average of 22x 
during its previous revenue upcycle where its 3-year revenue CAGR (2019-2021) was 
28%, vs. our 3-year revenue CAGR at 38% during 2025-2028E. 
Our TP implies 35% (ADR 54%) upside to the last close. As a result, we reiterate our Buy 
ratings for TSMC/ADR. We maintain our constructive view on TSMC’s long-term growth 
outlook as we believe the company’s solid technology leadership and execution better 
positions it vs. peers to capture the industry’s long-term structural growth 
opportunities, especially in AI.

### Page 9

Investment Thesis, Price Target Risks and Methodology

Investment Thesis - TSMC (2330.TW/TSM) 
TSMC is a leading global foundry company specializing in cutting-edge nodes. Its leading 
technology stance enables it to enjoy more than 60% revenue share in the global 
foundry market. We like TSMC as we believe its solid technology leadership and 
execution better position it vs. peers to capture the industry’s long-term structural 
growth opportunities, particularly in areas such as AI/5G/HPC/EV. In addition, valuation 
looks attractive in our view, with the shares trading at the mid-range of their 10-year 
trading history (P/E: 10-29x). Furthermore, we view TSMC as the key AI enabler among 
our Taiwan Semis coverage, thanks to its leadership stance in leading edge nodes and 
advanced packaging technology – CoWoS (chip on wafer on substrate). We believe TSMC 
will achieve its 25% revenue CAGR target for the next several years, driven primarily by 
increasing silicon content growth and AI/HPC demand, with LT GM to remain at 56%+. 
We are Buy rated.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - TSMC (2330.TW/TSM) 
Valuation methodology: We have a 12m TP of NT$3,300, which is derived by applying a 
target P/E multiple of 22x to our 2027E EPS. Our 22x target P/E is benchmarked against 
1.0stdv above its 5-year trading average. For the ADR (TSM), we have a 12m TP of 
US$660, based on a USD/TWD rate of 30.0 and an ADR premium of 20%. 
Key downside risks to our views: (1) further deterioration in end-demand recovery 
impacting capacity utilization; (2) slower customer node migrations; (3) slowdown in AI 
investment resulting in lower long-term semiconductor content growth; (4) poor 
yields/execution resulting in worse-than-expected profitability; (5) stronger 
competition resulting in ASP/profitability erosion; and (6) unfavorable FX trend or 
higher-than-expected cost increase weighing on the margin outlook.
15 October 2026   9

on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

### Page 1

Macquarie Equity Research
26 October 2026
Nan Ya Plastics
Mix upgrade accelerating
Key Points
• NPC reported Sep revenue up 55% YoY while net profit surged over 9x
YoY to TWD35.1bn, with Sep DPS above 2Q26 DPS.
• We expect a rising high-end product contribution to accelerate Low Dk/
Low CTE glass fibre sales next year.
• Our industry checks suggest that CCL supply will remain tight due to
glass fibre shortages.
• Upside to our 4Q26 forecast. NPC reported strong Sep earnings
following a required disclosure on unusual share-price movements.
Revenue rose 55% YoY to TWD34.4bn, while net profit surged over
9x to TWD35.1bn. Sep EPS reached TWD4.42, above 2Q26 quarterly
EPS of TWD3.37. Mgmt attributed the strength mainly to electronic
materials, including high-end CCL benefiting from AI demand. We
estimate Sept EBIT at TWD8–9bn, ~45% of 3Q26 EBIT, implying upside to
our TWD20.3bn 4Q EBIT estimate.
• Rising high-end CCL contribution in 2027/28. The company refuted
market rumours that its M9 material had failed customer validation,
noting that advanced CCL expansion remains on track, with new capacity
fully dedicated to high-end products. We expect stronger growth in Low
Dk/Low CTE glass fibres, with high-end products reaching 40% of the mix
vs 10% this year as CSP orders ramp up next year. We also expect M9
CCL to pass validation in 1H27.
• Q-Cloth and PTFE not ready yet. Our checks at Taiwan TPCA Show
2026 with glass fibre and CCL players suggest demand is multiple times
industry capacity, with glass-fibre supply the key bottleneck given long
capacity and weaving-machine ramp-up times. As producers shift
capacity towards Low Dk/T-Cloth, E-Cloth supply should remain tight. Q-
Cloth remains constrained by high costs and poor flexibility, while Low
Dk glass fibre with HC Resin can deliver similar performance. PTFE is
also unlikely to threaten glass fibre near term given its low mechanical
strength.
Earnings changes: No changes.
Valuation: We maintain our NT$506.00 TP based on an unchanged 20x
FY27E PER.
Catalysts: 2H26 high-end CCL ramp-up and capacity expansion, glass fibre
capacity additions, and stronger equity-method earnings from 2027.
Investment Thesis and Recommendation
We expect high-end CCL qualifications, mix upgrades and utilisation gains
to drive earnings growth, supported by recovering legacy businesses and
higher associate earnings.

Macquarie Equity Research Nan Ya Plastics
Key Risks to Investment Thesis
• Slower-than-expected AI data centre capex.
• Delays in, or failure of, high-end CCL qualification.
• Weaker-than-expected ASP and utilisation ramp.
• Normalisation of Middle East EG supply
• Earnings volatility at Nan Ya Technology and Formosa
Petrochemical.
Company Description
Nan Ya Plastics is a core member of the Formosa Plastics
Group. The company operates four business lines, including

plastics, chemicals, polyester, and electronic materials,
across Taiwan, China, and the US. Nan Ya also holds equity-
method stakes in Nan Ya Technology (2408 TT) and Formosa
Petrochemical (6505 TT), and consolidates PCB exposure
through Nan Ya PCB (8046 TT).

Key Quant Findings
The quant model currently holds a reasonably positive
view on Nan Ya Plastics. The strongest style exposure is
Price Momentum, indicating this stock has had strong
medium to long term returns which often persist into
the future. The weakest style exposure is Profitability,
indicating this stock is not efficiently converting
investments to earnings; proxied by ratios like ROE or ROA.

### Page 1

ab 27 October 2026
Global Research
First Read
Faraday Technology
New opportunities in advanced nodes
Q326 beat on turnkey strength and non-op gains; Q426 outlook positive
Q326 EPS of NT$1.07 came in above UBS/Street expectations mainly on  non-op gains.
Q326 sales came in at NT$3.66 bn (+11% QoQ), ahead of its original guidance but in
line with its comment  during Taiwan Summit with turnkey sales up 20% QoQ given
better support from OSAT and foundries, despite NRE declining 31% QoQ. Q326 GM

decreased 2.7ppt QoQ to 42.5% on higher turnkey mix, while  OPEX grew 9% QoQ  on
salary adjustment and higher bonus accrual. Non-op gain of NT$266mn mainly includes
valuation gains on an investee company. For Q426F, it guided sales  flat  QoQ remaining
at high levels with IP and NRE up QoQ, and turnkey down QoQ. GM is guided back to
~45%, reflecting normalized ASIC economics; OPEX should decline single digit QoQ.
2027 growth outlook supported by AI, advanced packaging and 12nm node

Management guided for double-digit sales growth in 2027, with all three businesses, IP ,
NRE and turnkey, expected to grow, driven by three key catalysts: AI, advanced
packaging and new UMC 12nm project contribution. For IP , growth should be
supported by higher royalties on foundry price hikes/customer capacity expansion and
initial  contribution from 12nm node; NRE should benefit from 2nm/4nm projects
entering peak recognition, with 3-4 ASIC tape-outs targeted in H127; while MP should

see growth in both MCU and ASIC as new projects gradually ramp. AI-related mix was at
8-9% of Q326 sales, and is expected to increase throughout 2027 alongside
accelerating advanced-packaging shipments. We expect 2027E sales to grow 37% YoY.
2nm design win opens new advanced-node and product opportunities
Faraday secured a 2nm GAA special purpose  ASIC project worth several tens of millions
of US dollars in NRE, which management described as its largest-ever design win and

most advanced project to date. The project spans front-end design and IP integration,
back-end design, customized 3D DRAM and advanced packaging, and marks Faraday's
first customized 3D DRAM and hybrid-bonding project. It expects to recognize its first
NRE milestone  in H127, followed by MP targeted within 1-2 years. Beyond 2nm, Faraday
is expanding into new applications including OCS, CXL and Si-cap, leveraging its
capabilities in advanced packaging, interposer design and ASIC integration. We believe

successful execution by Faraday and its foundry partner could strengthen Faraday's
positioning in advanced-node projects and create follow-on design-win opportunities.
Valuation: Raise PT to NT$200, stay Neutral
We raise    2026E EPS (reported) from NT$3.43 to NT$3.68 on better Q326, but lower
2027/28E EPS by 4%/1% to factor in softer CE demand and higher OPEX. We raise our
PT to NT$200 from NT$190 using 24x 2027E PE (vs prior 22x). We raise our multiple as

the recent 2nm project win should boost investor confidence, but stay Neutral as the
advanced nodes (4nm/2nm) turnkey revenue recognition depends on the foundry
partner's execution. We await clearer evidence before revisiting our assumptions.

Company Description
Faraday was founded in 1993, spun off from UMC's IP and semiconductor design service
department as the first IC design service company in Asia. It is an IC design and production
service provider that initially focused on specialty applications on mature nodes by partnering

with UMC and gradually upgraded to advanced nodes through working with Samsung. The
company had its IPO on the Taiwan Securities Exchange in 1999.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We value Faraday using PE methodology.
Downside risks include slower-than-expected advanced nodes migration, further delay of
advanced packaging ramp, and growing competition in specialty projects from China IC
design service companies.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

EQUITY: FABLESS
Global Unichip Corp   3443.TW 3443 TT
Global Markets Research
19 October 2026
Riding the next megatrend of SoIC; maintain Buy
Several key CSP projects in the pipeline to support growth until 2028-29F
Benefiting from SoIC — the next megatrend in advanced packaging, in our view
GUC shares have rallied ~100% since late July (vs the TAIEX up 11%), driven by a strong 
wave of consensus earnings upward revisions (Fig. 11). This is largely in line with our 
observations highlighted in our 2 Aug report that “consensus expectations were way too low.” 
We believe the Street is starting to acknowledge that there is a strong pipeline of CSP projects 
over the next 2-3 years, including Google CPU (GOOGL US, Not rated; revenue sharply 
higher in 2026 with momentum potentially sustaining into 2027F and 2028F as Axion 3 
doubles silicon content from Axion 2), as well as the other top three US hyperscalers’ ASIC 
and CPU projects along with Tesla’s (TSLA US, Not rated) AI5/AI6.5. GUC not only has 
secured projects from top US hyperscalers, but at the same time acquired additional 
meaningful customers with volume globally, e.g. China auto ASIC and US AI ASIC using a 
SoW approach. However, among all, the one that has caught our attention the most is the 
Google TPU v.10 compute die opportunity (though we believe the turnkey deal is yet to be 
finalized) — not only because the addressable size could be significant, but this business 
opportunity might originate from GUC’s G-link 3D die-on-die IP. (Fig. 1 Fig. 4) We raise our 
2026-28F EPS for GUC by 15%-37% to incorporate business upside from non-Axion projects, 
and raise TP to TWD9,600 (from TWD5,800), based on an unchanged 40x target P/E, while 
rolling forward valuation to the 2027-28F average EPS. We are yet to incorporate overseas 
convertible bonds (ECB) in our model/EPS estimates.
The rising importance of SoIC in advanced packaging should benefit GUC, given its 
leading investments in Glink-3D die-on-die IP among design service peers
SoIC is not new, but it was small before 2026 given limited adoption by AMD MI300 chip (AMD 
US, Not rated) and Apple M-series chip (AAPL US, Not rated). In 2026, nVidia’s (NVDA US, Not 
rated) demand for CPO using TSMC’s (2330 TT, Buy) COUPE (EIC on PIC stacking) has further 
lifted SoIC demand. In our June anchor report, we noted that nVidia’s Feynman could become 
the first GPU-on-GPU stacking SoIC (along with SiC carrier) AI chip. Since then, several US CSP 
ASIC projects have also planned to enable SoIC die stacking in their chip roadmap, according to 
our industry checks. GUC’s Glink-3D IP (see Fig. 1- Fig. 4) appears well positioned to ride this 
trend as it has developed this IP along with TSMC’s roadmap and currently a number of CSPs 
are using its IP to build their test chip – which in the long run could prove to be a good stepping 
stone for GUC to win more services, in our view. Besides, GUC’s SoIC IP would also be 
deployed with TSMC’s scale-up CPO customers (e.g. AyarLabs, Fig. 5).

### Page 3

Company profile
Established in 1998, GUC is one of the leading design service companies in Taiwan with self-developed IPs. TSMC is GUC's single-largest
shareholder with a 35% share.
Valuation Methodology
Our TP of TWD9,600 is based on 40x 2027-28F average EPS (40x P/E is its historical mid level). The benchmark index for the stock is TAIEX.
Risks that may impede the achievement of the target price
Downside risks include 1) fierce ASIC competition, 2) slowdown of key projects and lack of capacity, and 3) more insourcing trend from customers

ESG
GUC has six major ESG strategies to achieve the goals of sustainable development, including corporate governance, innovative R&D, partnership
prosperity, environmental sustainability, friendly workplace, and social participation.

Definition of Nomura Group's equity research rating system and sectors
The rating system is a relative system, indicating expected performance against a specific benchmark identified for each individual stock, 
subject to limited management discretion. An analyst’s target price is an assessment of the current intrinsic fair value of the stock based on an 
appropriate valuation methodology determined by the analyst. Valuation methodologies include, but are not limited to, discounted cash flow 
analysis, expected return on equity and multiple analysis. Analysts may also indicate expected absolute upside/downside relative to the stated 
target price, defined as (target price - current price)/current price.

SECTORS
A 'Bullish' stance, indicates that the analyst expects the sector to outperform the Benchmark during the next 12 months. A 'Neutral' stance, 
indicates that the analyst expects the sector to perform in line with the Benchmark during the next 12 months. A 'Bearish' stance, indicates that 
the analyst expects the sector to underperform the Benchmark during the next 12 months. Sectors that are labelled as 'Not rated' or shown as 
'N/A' are not assigned ratings. Benchmarks are as follows: United States: S&P 500; Europe: Dow Jones STOXX 600; Global Emerging 
Markets (ex-Asia): MSCI Emerging Markets ex-Asia. Japan/Asia ex-Japan: Sector ratings are not assigned.

Target Price
A Target Price, if discussed, indicates the analyst’s forecast for the share price with a 12-month time horizon, reflecting in part the analyst's 
estimates for the company's earnings. The achievement of any target price may be impeded by general market and macroeconomic trends, and 
by other risks related to the company or the market, and may not occur if the company's earnings differ from estimates.

營運展望 
1. 2H26 營運動能來自 AMD Helios 伺服器專案開始放量：針對 Meta、Oracle 與 
OpenAI 等客戶的 AMD Helios 專案，已由去年的研發與小量送樣階段，於今
年正式啟動大量生產。儘管受限於保密協議無法透露單一客戶細節，但整體專
案規模與出貨量將迎來顯著成長。2026 年上半年營收約 41.0 億元，第三季單
季貢獻即達 40.3 億元 ， 推升前三季累計營收至 81.3 億元 。 受惠下半年強勁拉
貨，全年營收預估將突破百億大關並創下歷史新高，年增率上看五成。 
2. 與 NVIDIA 深化儲存專案合作，共同開發下次世代應用：跟 NVIDIA 除延續
多年來在人工智慧資料處理器 (Data Processing Unit, DPU AI) 儲存市場合作，
更於今年  GTC 大會深化與  NVIDIA 策略夥伴關係。雙方正緊密圍繞最新  
Vera Rubin 平台，共同開發下一代鍵值快取 (Knowledge Base Cache, KB Cache)  
在內容管理系統 (Content Management System , CMS ) 的應用。與 NVIDIA 次
世代合作案正處聯合開發階段，將聚焦提供高階 AI 儲存完整解決方案，其市
場份額與營收貢獻將隨 NVIDIA 的市場推廣逐步發酵。 
3. 2027 年既有業務穩健成長 ， 新興 AI 業務加速拓展：在三大事業部同步成長 ，
以及歐洲 、 東南亞 、 印度與中國等非北美市場大幅貢獻的帶動下 ， 預期 2027 年
仍將延續五成的強勁營收成長幅度。預期標準品、AI 儲存與 CSP 三大事業群
的營收年增率將呈現均衡且穩定成長。其中，標準品於各區域市場皆持續貢獻
穩定營收，AI 儲存受惠於整體應用規模擴增趨勢而由專責團隊推動成長，CSP 
專案則依既有進度維持穩健的營收佔比 。公司整體毛利率穩定維持在 25 ~ 30% 
區間，未來各項產品銷售與營運規劃，也將持續以穩住此毛利率水準作為首要
目標。 
4. 三大事業部門業務能見度佳，明年營運獲利可創新高：三大事業部同步成長，
Server & Storage 標準品業績規模持續擴大，出貨量顯著成長。AI Storage 出貨
快速攀升，掌握重點客戶成長商機。CSP 專案持續拓展，AMD Helios 進入大
規模量產階段 。合庫預估 2026 年營收 122.8 億元 、+63.4% YoY， 毛利率 28.2%。
營業利益 18.7 億元、+112.8% YoY，營業利益率 15.2%，歸屬母公司稅後淨利 
17.1 億元，每股盈餘 39.73 元。 
5. ESG (營邦在 2026 年上櫃公司治理評鑑結果為 81 ~ 100%)：為實踐企業社會
責任，並促進經濟、社會與環境生態之平衡及永續發展，營邦制定「永續發展
實務守則」 與 「永續資訊管理作業程序」 ，將永續做為公司營運決策的一環。透
過檢視治理、環境與社會的管理架構，營邦運用永續策略促進創新，以符合平
衡環境、社會及公司治理發展之國際趨勢，並透過企業公民擔當，提升國家經
濟貢獻 ， 改善員工 、 社區 、 社會之生活品質 ， 促進以永續發展為本的競爭優勢 。

載板、晶圓客戶、OSAT 客戶均樂觀 
 2Q26 毛利率 25.87%，低於預期，主要仍是中國毛利率拖累，2Q26 
EPS 2.02 元:迅得 2Q26 EPS 2.02 元，與元富預估 2 元差異不大。
2Q26 營收 18.24 億元， +5%QOQ，+12%YOY，2Q26 毛利 率
25.87%，低於元富預估的 26.5%，主要是中國地區仍有低價競爭影
響，本業獲利達成率 99%，業外收益挹注下，2Q26 EPS 2.02 元。 
 交期拉長，2027 年預計擴產 40%，2027 年營收挑戰 100 億元:載板
客戶積極擴產，載板三雄欣興、南電、景碩均為公司客戶，近年積極
布局轉戰載板市場的臻鼎亦為客戶，由於載板面積增大， 技術難度提
升，為了維持相同生產效率須提高自動化資本支出，故以過往來看，
自動化設備佔客戶資本之約為 5%，現在提升至 15%。晶圓製造及
OSAT 隨客戶台灣及海外積極擴產， 交期過去 4-6 個月現在已經拉長
到 6-8 個月，載板、晶圓客戶、OSAT 客戶均樂觀，2027 年營收挑
戰 100 億元， 預計2027 年將新增產能 40%，以支應客戶未來需求。 
 維持買進評等，目標價 220 元：3Q26~2027 年營收逐季增長，新產
能逐步開出， 故維持買進，預估2026 年 EPS 8.51 元，2027 年 EPS 
11.40 元(原估 10.95 元)，目標價 220 元。(2027 PER 20x)

迅得為自動化設備供應商 
 迅得為自動化設備供應商 
迅得機械成立於 1999 年，主要產品為印刷電路板、軟板、光電及半導體物流系
統之自動化解決方案，2025 年營收比重可區分為 PCB 事業部(41%)、半導體-
載板事業部 13%、半導體-晶圓事業部 12%、半導體-封裝事業部 32%，面板事
業部 2%。主要客戶包括欣興、南亞、景碩、台郡、臻鼎、日月光、台積電、友
達、群創等。

迅得九月營收 6.95 億元，+14%MOM，+31%YOY，營收達成率 104%，優於
預期。累計 3Q26 營收 19.45 億元，+6.6%QOQ，+16.7%YOY，預估 3Q26 毛
利率 27%，預估 3Q26 EPS 2.26 元。展望第四季，原估 4Q26 營收 19.63 億元，
現因 3Q26 營收優於預期，故上修 4Q26 營收至 20.42 億元，維持 4Q26 營收
+5%QOQ 之預估，故預估 4Q26 營收 20.42 億元，+5%QOQ，+26.4%YOY，
毛利率 27.5%，預估 4Q26 EPS 2.47 元。

個股報告

載板 、 晶圓客戶 、OSAT 客戶均樂
觀，2027 年營收挑戰 100 億元

載板、晶圓客戶、OSAT 客戶均樂觀，2027 年營收挑戰 100 億元 
迅得 1H26 營收占比為 PCB 4 1%，載板 16%，晶圓製造 16%，封裝 25%，其
他 2%。PCB 主要產品以自動上下料、物件搬運及智慧倉儲為主； IC 載板亦為
自動上下料較多，整廠規畫約佔比 60%，單機搬運 20%，倉儲 15~20%；半導
體晶圓廠則以倉儲為主，台灣部分標準倉儲較多，微型倉儲較少， 海外部分目
前未出貨標準倉儲，僅供應微型倉儲，主要是安裝時間及人力配置考量；封裝
OSAT 廠主要供應倉儲佔比 60~70%，自動化搬運約 30%。

載板客戶積極擴產，載板三雄欣興、南電、景碩均為公司客戶，近年積極布局
轉戰載板市場的臻鼎亦為客戶，以欣興資本支出來看，2026 年 340 億元，2027
年長交期採購設備訂單金額 92 億元 ， 均將投入桃園楊梅廠及光復廠 ； 景碩2026
年資本支出 140 億元(原本是 80 億元)，將投入於六廠建置及設備導入，2027
年資本支出為 160 億元(原本是 100 億元)， 擴產進度明確 。 由於載板面積增大，
技術難度提升 ，為了維持相同生產效率須提高自動化資本支出 ，故以過往來看，
自動化設備佔客戶資本之約為 5%，現在提升至 15%。

晶圓製造部分，隨 客戶台灣及海外積極擴產，因目前現有自動化廠商均無單一
廠商可獨立供足客戶所需 自動化設備量體，故每個案件 公司均有相對應配比，
以海外廠來說，考量人力成本及安裝時間公司僅提供微型倉儲為主。 OSAT 部
分，客戶拉貨持續積極，半導體業務維持樂觀。以交期來看，過去 4~6 個月現
在已經拉長到 6-8 個月，載板、晶圓客戶、OSAT 客戶均樂觀，2027 年營收挑
戰 100 億元，預計 2027 年將新增產能 40%，以支應客戶未來需求。

維持買進，目標價 220 元

維持買進評等，目標價 220 元 
3Q26~2027 年營收逐季增長 ， 新產能逐步開出 ， 故維持買進 ， 預估2026 年 EPS 
8.51 元，2027 年 EPS 11.40 元(原估 10.95 元)， 目標價220 元。(2027 PER 20x)

風險提示 
出貨不如預期，ASP 不如預期。

주가 추이

3Q 큰 폭의 판매 감소, 매출/영업이익 시장 기대치 하회 예상 
3Q26 동사 매출액과 영 업 이 익 은 각각 44.6조원(-4.5% 이하 Y oY)과 2.4조원
(-5.4%) 기록하며 시장 기대치 하회 전망. 3Q 매출액은 글로벌 판매 부진 지속
과 환율 하락, 그리고 친환경차 판매 성장 둔화 등의 영향에 기인. 특히, 3Q 판
매량은 918.7만대(-11.5% 이하 Y oY)로 전년 동기 대비 큰 폭 감소하며 매출
액에 부정적 영향. 이는 7월과 8월 발생한 파업 영향으로 약 5.5만대 규모의 생
산 물량 차질이 발 생 한 부분에 기인. 파업은 기존 물량/믹스 개선에 기 여 했 던 
글로벌 HEV 판매도 둔화(+3.4%)시키면서 매출에 부정적 영향을 심화. 
3Q 수 익 성 은 4~5월 상승했던 원자재 투입에 따른 원가 상승 영향과 친환경차 
판매 감소에 따른 부정적 믹스 영향 불 가 피. 이에 시장 기대치인 3.0조원을 크
게 하회할 전망. 다만, 기말 환율이 1,356원/$ 수준으로 낮아지면서 판매보증비 
외화환산손익 영향은 다소 개선(0.4%p) 예상되며, 관세 영향 또한 전년 동기 
대비 약 1조원(2.1%p) 감소하면서 수익성 악화 영향을 일부 만 회 할 전망.  
’27년 지연된 신차효과 가시화로 반등 여력 충분 
4Q부터는 3Q 파업 영향 등으로 지연된 신차 확대 본격화되고, 현지 수요 높은 
차종 중심의 물량 확대를 통해 매출 회복 및 이익 성장 가시화가 기대. 아 반 떼
와 투싼 FMC 확대로 국내 및 해외 판매 둔화가 점진적 만회되고, 지난 9월부
터 터키에서 아이오닉3 생산이 본격화되면서 유럽에서의 시장 부진 회복이 기
대. 또한, GV80과 G80 HEV 모델 출시로 내년부터 국내/북미 중심 판매 확대
되며 올해 부진했던 물량/믹스 개선에 기여할 것으로 기대. ’27년 HMGMA에
서 HEV 신차 생산 투입 예정되어 있어 상 대 적 으 로 높은 수요가 지속되는 북미
에서의 HEV 현지 생산 확대로 가동률 증 가 와 관세 부담 완화도 수익성 개선에 
기여할 것으로 기대. 
부정적 환율 고려 필요하나 ’27년 강한 증익 기대  
’27년 동사 영 업 이 익 추 정 치 는 12.4조원(+22.3%)으로 ’26년 감 익 에 서 ’27년 
증익 전환 예상. 최근 환율 추세 고려 시 내년 상 반 기 까 지 부정적인 환율 영향 
확대 가 능 성 존재하나, 기존 노후 차 종 에 서 현지 수요 대응 가능한 신차 중심 
생산/판매 확대로 물량/믹스 개선되며 부 정 적 환율 영향 상쇄 기대.  
12M EPS 추정치 하향(-11.8%)과 상반기 적 용 한 코 스 피 프리미엄 제거(-
10%)에 따라 목 표 주 가 를 670,000원으로 하향. 그러나, 연 내 RMAC 운영 
성 과 와 RA 설립, 12 월 모셔널 자 율 주 행 상용화와 웨 이 모向 로 보 택 시 
양산/공급 등 미래 사 업 에 서 의 가 시 성 도 여전히 기대 가능한 부분.

[ Compliance Notice ] (공표일: 2026 년 10월 8 일) 
이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 8

현대차 (005380) [한화리서치]

8 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 9 월 30 일)

### Page 1

www.imeritz.com

삼성바이오로직스 (207940)

Company Brief 
제약/바이오 
Analyst 김준영 
junyoung.kim@meritz.co.kr 
RA 김현비 
hyunbee.kim@meritz.co.kr 외형 성장 지속, 수익성 개선은 시간이 필요

3Q26 Review  
3Q26 연결 기준 매출액 1조 4,903억원(YoY +18.5%, QoQ +12.8%), 영업이익 
6,401억원(YoY +1.0%, QoQ +9.2%)으로 컨센서스를 각각 9.8%, 4.7% 상회했다. 
기존 1 ~ 4공장 풀가동 및 미국 Rockville 공장 매출 약 1,000억원과 5공장 PPQ 
물량 인식이 더해지며 외형 성장을 견인했다. 반면 신규 생산시설의 초기 비용 및 제
품 믹스 영향으로 OPM은 42.9%를 기록하며 전년 동기 대비 7.5%p 하락했다. 동
사는 연간 매출 성장률 가이던스를 기존 +15~20%에서 +20%로 상향했다. 당사는 
4분기 Rockville 공장 매출 기여와 기존 공장의 높은 가동률을 감안하여 연간 매출
액 5조 6,135억원(YoY +23.2%)을 예상한다.

신규 생산거점의 외형 확대, 이익 기여는 좀 더 긴 호흡으로 
5공장은 2Q26부터 PPQ 생산을 본격화했으나 상업 생산에 따른 실질적인 이익 기
여는 2027년 하반기에 확대될 전망이다. Rockville 공장은 3Q와 4Q 각각 약 
1,000억원의 매출이 예상되며 2027년 분기별 매출도 유사한 수준을 유지할 것으로 
전망한다. 다만 영업이익 기여까지는 시간이 더 소요될 것으로 예상되어 2027년 이
익 성장은 미국 공장보다 5공장 Ramp-up이 주도할 전망이다.

투자의견 Buy, 적정주가 200만원 유지 
삼성바이오로직스에 대해 투자의견 Buy, 적정주가 200만원을 유지한다. 고객사 수주 
문의가 회복되는 가운데 대형 계약 협의가 진행 중이며 6공장 역시 연말 또는 내년 
초 착공을 목표로 하고 있다. 다만 신규 수주가 실제 계약으로 이어지는 시점과 5공
장의 상업 생산 전환 속도가 중기 실적의 핵심 변수로 판단한다. 추가 대형 수주 확
보 시 5공장 이후의 성장 가시성도 높아질 것으로 예상한다. 
PolyPeptide 인수는 펩타이드 CDMO로 사업을 확장한다는 점에서 긍정적이다. 인
수 완료 이후 다음 분기부터 연결 매출 인식이 가능하나 유의미한 영업이익 기여까지
는 최소 2년 이상 소요될 전망이다. PolyPeptide 인수는 현재 추정치에서 반영하지 
않았으며 향후 인수 완료 시 연결 OPM 희석 가능성을 감안할 필요가 있다. 한편 최
근 법원 항고심에서 배양부터 정제까지의 생산공정이 쟁의행위 기간에도 정상 수행되
어야 할 보전작업으로 인정되면서 파업에 따른 생산 차질 우려는 완화될 것으로 판단
한다. 노사 협상은 여전히 진행 중이나 생산 안정성에 대한 고객사 우려가 낮아질 경
우 신규 수주에도 긍정적으로 작용할 전망이다. 
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 22

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준)

투자등급 관련사항 (2023년 8월 4일부터 기준 변경 시행)  투자의견 비율 
기업 향후 12 개월간 추천기준일 직전 1 개월간 평균종가대비 추천종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미  투자의견 비율 
추천기준일  
직전 1 개월간 
종가대비 3 등급 
Buy 추천기준일 직전 1 개월(20 거래일)간 평균종가대비 +20% 이상  매수 91.9% 
Hold 추천기준일 직전 1 개월(20 거래일)간 평균종가대비 -20% 이상 ~ +20% 미만  중립 8.2% 
Sell 추천기준일 직전 1 개월(20 거래일)간 평균종가대비 -20% 미만  매도 0.0% 
산업 시가총액기준 산업별 시장비중 대비 보유비중의 변화를 추천  2026년 9 월 30 일 기준으로  
최근 1 년간 금융투자상품에 대하여 
공표한 최근일 투자등급의 비율 추천기준일  
시장지수대비 3 등급

BUSINESS CREATIONS AND BANKRUPTCIES IN FRANCE: DOUBLE
-
DIGIT GROWTH IN BUSINESS CREATIONS,
LOWER GROWTH IN BANKRUPTCIES,
AND STABLE NON
-
PERFORMING LOANS
ECO CHARTS | BUSINESS CREATIONS AND BANKRUPTCIES IS A NEW PUBLIC
ATION AIMED AT PROVIDING AN OVERVIEW OF THE DYNAMICS
OF BUSINESS CREATIONS AND BANKRUPTCIES IN FRANCE, AS WELL AS THE
RISKS ASSOCIATED WITH THE LATTER. WE WILL PUBLISH
REGULAR UPDATES TO THIS PUBLICATION ON A QUARTERLY BASIS.

### Page 2

Double
-
digit growth in business creations, a slowdown in bankruptcies and stable non
-
performing loans
2
ECOCHARTS
ECONOMIC STUDIES
•
Business
creations
rose
by
12
%
y/y
in
the
first
eight
months
of
2026
,
marking
an
acceleration
compared
with
the
growth
seen
in
2025
(+
5
.
3
%
)
.
•
This
increase
was
driven
by
sectors
associated
with
new
information
and
communication
technologies,
where
the
trend
is
gaining
momentum
.
•
Meanwhile,
business
bankruptcies

rose
by
5
.
3
%
y/y
from
January
to
July,
according
to
the
data
from
the
Banque
de
France
(vs
+
3
.
6
%
y/y
in
2025
)
.
After
a
more
pronounced
rise
in
the
first
quarter
(+
8
%
y/y),
this
increase
has
since
slowed
.
This
moderation
could
continue,
as,
according
to
Altares,
payment
delays
have
decreased
to
an
average
of
12
.
3
days
in
the
second
quarter
of
2026
(
14
.
1
days
a
year
earlier),
compared
with
the
European
average
of
13
.
1
days
.
•
The

rise
in
business
bankruptcies
is
most
pronounced
in
the
services
sector,
which
has
simultaneously
seen
an
increase
in
business
creations
.
This
trend
is
clear
in
the
retail
sector,
with
a
13
%
increase
in
business
creations
since
the
beginning
of
2026
,
while
business
bankruptcies
are
up
3
%
.
•
In
construction
and
manufacturing,
business
bankruptcies
have
been
declining
since
the
second
quarter
of
2026
.
•
The
level
of
the
average
ratio
of
non
-

performing
loans
is
moderately
high
and
has
remained
stable
for
the
past
year
(
4
.
0
%
of
total
loans
and
advances
in
Q
2
2026
)
.
More
cyclical
than
in
other
sectors
,
the
ratio
of
non
-
performing
loans
in
construction
reached
its
highest
level
since
2019
at
9
.
5
%
.
•
The
share
of
underperforming
loans,
but
not
yet
non
-
performing
(in
Stage
2
,
according
to
IFRS
9
terminology),
which
provides
an
indication
of
the
future
trend
in
non
-
performing

loans,
is
stable,
standing
at
15
.
4
%
q/q,
and
remains
below
its
all
-
time
high
(
16
.
1
%
)
.
•
Despite
a
significant
rise
since
2021
,
the
economic
weight
of
bankruptcies
–
which
allows
them
to
be
weighted
by
a
proxy
for
company
size
–
remains,
on
average,
below
its
Q
1
2025
peak
(
0
.
53
compared
with
0
.
61
,
respectively)
.
However,
the
economic
weight
of
bankrupted
small
businesses
stands
at
a
historically
high
level
(
2
.
11
)
.
For
more information, please refer to our publication:

hart of the

Document 304: France’s unrest adds to an already toxic economic mix

Source PDF: all report pdf/France’s unrest adds to an already toxic economic mix.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-10-20
Pages: 2

### Page 1

Décembre 2018 53
Conjoncture française
Impact macroéconomique du mouvement des gilets jaunes :
une estimation difficile à ce stade
Estimer l’impact économique du mouvement des
gilets jaunes est un exercice difficile et incertain à la
date de finalisation de cetteNote de conjoncture (13
décembre 2018). Ce mouvement, qui a pris une
forme assez inédite, a en effet commencé à la
mi–novembre, soit relativement récemment, et se
poursuit encore au moment où s’achève la rédaction

de cette Note. Très peu d’indicateurs
macroéconomiques sont donc connus à ce stade.
Centré au départ sur la hausse des prix du carburant
automobile, le mouvement des gilets jaunes s’est
ensuite élargi à d’autres revendications, notamment
sur le pouvoir d’achat. Il a surtout pris la forme de
blocages routiers, mais aussi de manifestations
parfois émaillées de violence, à Paris comme en
province et outre–mer.
Si ce mouvement ne comporte pas de précédent

historique directement comparable, on peut tout de
même tenter de lister les canaux par lesquels il est
susceptible d’affecter l’activité économique.
Un impact probable sur la consommation,
notamment de services
Du côté de la production industrielle, les blocages de
routes et de dépôts pétroliers ont pu engendrer des
retards de livraison et cert aines entreprises peuvent
pâtir de problèmes d’approvisionnement.
Néanmoins, ces blocages semblent à ce stade d’une

ampleur moindre que ceux qui avaient affecté
l’activité économique pendant, par exemple, les
grèves massives de décembre 1995, lesquelles
avaient ôté environ 0,2 point à la croissance
trimestrielle du PIB.
En revanche, le mouvement des gilets jaunes est de
nature à pénaliser la consommation, localement
assez durement, et par plusieurs canaux. Tout
d’abord, les blocages, notamment routiers, ont pu
conduire les ménages à reporter ou annuler certaines

dépenses. Les dépenses en biens peuvent certes être
en partie reportées. Mais cet effet de report est en
général moindre pour les dépenses en services (en
particulier dans l’hébergement–restauration, les loisirs,
les transports) qui pourraient donc connaître des pertes
sèches plus marquées.
Un effet sur le tourisme
Car, aux effets directs des blocages, s’ajoute le
caractère anxiogène des s cènes de violence qui ont
marqué le mouvement, en particulier les samedis 24

novembre et, plus encore, 1er et 8 décembre. Certaines
se sont déroulées dans des lieux symboliques du pays et
hautement touristiques (avenue des Champs–Elysées et
Arc de Triomphe à Paris). On peut donc s’attendre à ce
qu’elles pénalisent les activités liées au tourisme.
La France a déjà connu des épisodes émeutiers, par
exemple en novembre 2005. Les données disponibles
suggèrent néanmoins que ces événements n’ont sans
doute pas eu d’impact significatif sur l’activité

touristique. Celles-ci concernaient alors la région
parisienne, mais pas le cœur touristique de la capitale.
À l’inverse, les attentats de novembre 2015 à Paris,
évènements de nature certes très différente, ont pesé
pendant plusieurs mois sur le tourisme, ôtant
0,2 point de PIB à la croissance annuelle de 2016 (cf.
Insee, 2016 et Beatriz, 2018).
À titre illustratif, la chronique des glissements annuels
des nuitées dans les hôtels figure sur le graphique

ci–dessous pour la période 2015–2016. La
fréquentation touristique dans l’hôtellerie a nettement
baissé après les attentats de novembre 2015
(graphique ). C’est – logiquement – la clientèle
étrangère qui a été la plus touchée. L ’attentat du 14
juillet 2016 à Nice a sans doute eu un effet du même
type, même s’il a été moins marqué. Au moins deux
autres évènements ont vraisemblablement eu aussi un
impact sur cette série durant la période considérée : les

manifestations du printemps 2016, dont la violence
ponctuelle n’était néanmoins pas comparable à celle du
1er décembre 2018, et, à l’inverse, l’Euro de football
organisé en France en juin et juillet 2016.

### Page 2

Conjoncture française
54 Note de conjoncture
En novembre 2018, la confiance des ménages
dans la situation économique a nettement
baissé
À côté des principaux indicateurs macroéconomiques,
les enquêtes de conjoncture auprès des entreprises et
des ménages permettent d’avoir une idée de la
manière dont les acteurs économiques envisagent leur
situation à venir.
Mi–décembre, les résultats des enquêtes de
conjoncture de l’Insee auprès des entreprises ne sont

pas encore disponibles pour la période postérieure aux
manifestations émaillées de violence. Le mouvement
des gilets jaunes ayant commencé le 17 novembre, les
chefs d’entreprise n’ont pas pu les prendre en compte
dans leur réponse à l’enquête de novembre.
L ’enquête de conjoncture auprès des ménages affiche
quant à elle une nette dégradation de l’indicateur
synthétique de confiance en novembre. Toutes les
composantes de la confiance sont en baisse marquée.

En particulier, le solde sur l’opportunité de faire des
achats importants chute en novembre ; or ce solde
peut être relié à la consommation future des ménages
(cf. éclairage : Que disent les ménages sur leur
perception des évolutions de leur niveau de vie dans
les enquêtes de conjoncture ?). Il laisse donc présager
une consommation relativement peu dynamique au
quatrième trimestre 2018, trimestre pourtant marqué
par des mesures fiscales (baisse de la taxe d’habitation

et des cotisations salariales) venant augmenter le
pouvoir d’achat des ménages.
Le mouvement social pourrait également
peser sur l’emploi
Enfin, le ralentissement de l’activité lié au mouvement
des gilets jaunes pourrait également affecter la
dynamique des créations d’emploi. Par exemple,
certaines embauches de contrats courts ou d’intérim
prévus dans les magasins à l’occasion des fêtes de fin
d’année pourraient finalement ne pas se concrétiser à

cause des blocages.
Au total, le scénario de cetteNote de conjoncture fait
l’hypothèse que le mouvement des gilets jaunes
pourrait ôter 0,1 point à la croissance du PIB au
quatrième trimestre 2018, via les secteurs d’activité
sans doute principalement touchés : commerce,
hébergement, restauration, transports… Cette
estimation est néanmoins soumise à beaucoup
d’aléas, ne serait–ce que sur la durée du mouvement.
Bibliographie
Insee (2017), « En 2017, le tourisme ne pèserait plus

sur la croissance française, après avoir coûté 0,2
point en 2016 », éclairage de laNote de conjoncture
de juin 2017.
Beatriz (2018), « Le retour des touristes en 2017 a
contribué pour 0,2 point à la croissance française »,
encadré de l’édition 2018 de L’économie française,
comptes et dossiers (Insee). /c110

Document 378: The Institute Employment Report_ September 2026 Bank of America Institute analysis shows slower payroll growth, declining unemployment payments and continued strength in lower-inco

Source PDF: all report pdf/The Institute Employment Report_ September 2026 Bank of America Institute analysis shows slower payroll growth, declining unemployment payments and continued strength in lower-inco_223e3a6a.pdf
Report source: Bank of America
Date: 2026-10-01

13025075     1

Economy 
The Institute Employment Report: September 2026 
07 October 2026

Key takeaways 
• Job growth softened further in September, according to Bank of America's deposit data, with growth of 1.4% year-over-year 
(YoY), from 1.5% in August. But falling unemployment payments in September indicate a relatively healthy overall picture. 
• Lower-income households' wage growth remains strongest, despite a narrowing gap. After-tax wage growth eased to 4.5% YoY 
for lower-income households in September, while rising to 3.8% and 3.7% for middle- and higher-income households, 
respectively. 
• Lower tax withholdings and a relative strengthening in the younger/lower-income end of the labor market are driving this story. 
Additionally, average hours have risen in some lower-paid industries, which is also boosting pay packets.

Job growth slowed in September, but unemployment trends are benign  
Our estimate of job growth – which uses Bank of America deposit data to track changes in the number of customer accounts 
receiving a paycheck (see Methodology) – slowed further in September, to 1.4% year-over-year (YoY) from 1.5% YoY in August. 
The data can be noisy, partly due to seasonal variation and differences in pay-period timing, but in our view, the latest reading is 
consistent with the easing in payrolls growth seen in the September Bureau of Labor Statistics (BLS) data (Exhibit 1). 
Still, despite slower hiring, there is little sign of a rise in joblessness in Bank of America data. Unemployment payments into 
customer accounts showed a YoY decline in September (Exhibit 2). So, overall, labor market trends appear resilient.   
Exhibit 1: September payroll growth showed further slowing in 
Bank of America deposit account data

(BLS) and Automatic Data Processing (ADP) (monthly, YoY)

After-tax wage growth cooled for lower-income, but rose for other cohorts 
Lower-income wage growth eased to 4.5% YoY in September from 4.7% YoY in August, while wage growth for middle- and 
higher-income households rose to 3.8% and 3.7% YoY, respectively. Overall, these changes were modest, and lower-income 
households are continuing to see faster after-tax wage growth than other cohorts, a notable turnaround from the “K-shaped” 
pattern we observed over much of 2025 and 2026 (Exhibit 3). 
Exhibit 3: Lower-income wage growth remains strongest, despite a narrowing gap 
After-tax wage and salary growth by household income terciles, based on Bank of America aggregated consumer deposit account data (three-month moving 
average, YoY%, SA)

Source: Bank of America internal data 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
In our view, the pick-up in lower- and middle-income households’ after-tax wage growth continues to reflect the impact of the 
One Big Beautiful Bill Act (OBBA) in reducing tax withholdings, alongside an underlying rise in job-to-job changes at the 
younger/lower-income end of the labor market (for more, read: The Institute Employment Report: August 2026). Consistent with 
that underlying pick up, BLS data shows a solid YoY rise in average hours worked in several relatively lower-paid industries, such 
as manufacturing, retail and other services. Hourly workers in these industries are likely seeing rising paychecks as a result 
(Exhibit 4).    
Exhibit 4: Some relatively lower-paid industries have seen a rise in hours worked

Methodology  
Selected Bank of America transaction data is used to inform the macroeconomic views expressed in this report and should be considered in the 
context of other economic indicators and publicly available information. In certain instances, the data may provide directional and/or predictive 
value. The data used is not comprehensive; it is based on aggregated and anonymized selections of Bank of America data and may reflect a 
degree of selection bias and limitations on the data available. 
Any payments data represents aggregated spend from US Retail, Preferred, Small Business and Wealth Management clients with a deposit 
account or credit card. Aggregated spend include total credit card, debit card, ACH, wires, bill pay, business/peer-to-peer, cash, and checks.  
Any Small Business payments data represents aggregate spend from Small Business clients with a deposit account or a Small Business credit 
card. Payroll payments data include channels such as ACH (automated clearing house), bill pay, checks and wire. Bank of America per Small 
Business client data represents activity spending from active Small Business clients with a deposit account or a Small Business credit card and 
at least one transaction in each month. Small businesses in this report include business clients within Bank of America and generally defined as 
under $5mm in annual sales revenue.  
Unless otherwise stated, data is not adjusted for seasonality, processing days or portfolio changes, and may be subject to periodic revisions.  
Bank of America aggregated credit/debit card spending per household includes spending from active US households only. Only consumer card 
holders making a minimum of five transactions a month are included in the dataset. Spending from corporate cards are excluded. Data 
regarding merchants who receive payments are identified and classified by the Merchant Categorization Code (MCC) defined by financial 
services companies. The data are mapped using proprietary methods from the MCCs to the North American Industry Classification System 
(NAICS), which is also used by the Census Bureau, in order to classify spending data by subsector. Spending data may also be classified by other 
proprietary methods not using MCCs.  
Lower, middle and higher household income cuts in Bank of America credit and debit card spending per household, and consumer deposit 
account data are based on quantitative estimates of each households’ income. These quantitative estimates are bucketed according to terciles, 
with a third of households placed in each tercile periodically. The lowest tercile represents ‘lower income’, the middle tercile represents ‘middle 
income’ and the highest tercile ‘higher income’. The income thresholds between these terciles will move over time, reflecting any number of 
factors that impact income, including general wage inflation, changes in social security payments and individual households’ income. The 
income and tercile in which a household is categorised are periodically re-assessed.  
Generations, if discussed, are defined as follows: Gen Z, born after 1995; Younger Millennials: born between 1989-1995; Older Millennials: born 
between 1978-1988; Gen Xers: born between 1965-1977; Baby Boomer: 1946-1964; Traditionalists: pre-1946. 
Estimate of payroll growth from Bank of America internal data is based on the change in customer accounts receiving a paycheck in the month. 
An adjustment is made for the difference between overall population growth and customer account growth. Occasionally historic payments can 
be reclassified, leading to revisions in the series.  
An estimate of bonus growth from Bank of America deposit data is calculated by looking at customers who have received an inbound ACH 
payroll transaction in the last two years. From this sample an estimate of bonuses is derived by looking for payroll transactions which are over 
50% higher than the median regular payroll payments received by the customer. Of these payments only those that were received around the 
same time in each of the last two years are selected.  
The job-to-job change rate (j2j rate) is defined as the proportion of customers with an identified change in their employer as a proportion of 
the total number of customers with employment income. We estimate the median pay rise associated with a j2j change using the pay in the 
first three months of the new job compared to the same three months a year ago. 
Additional information about the methodology used to aggregate the data is available upon request.

### Page 2

UNITED STATES 
The US economy is expected to grow above its potential in 2026, with an average annual growth rate of 2. 2%, close to the 2025 rate (2.1%) . 
This apparent resilience to the uncertainty, tariff and energy shocks, together with above-trend productivity growth, masks K-shaped growth, 
driven by investment linked to AI-optimism and consumption by the wealthiest amid a wealth effect (historically high stock market valuations). 
Inflation overshooting is set to continue (3.4% in 2026) at least through 2028, largely because of the rise in oil prices and tariffs – although the 
impact of these latter appears to be less significant than expected. The labour market is showing clear signs of improvement that should bring 
the unemployment rate down toward 4.0%. Given this shift in price and employment risks amid dynamic growth, the FOMC proceeded with a 
first rate hike at September’s meeting and we expect two further 25bp increases in December 2026 and January 2027, bringing the Fed Funds 
target range at 4,25% - 4,50%.

CHINA 
Economic growth slowed to 4.3% y/y in Q2 2026 vs. 5.0% in Q1. It is projected to reach 4.6% in 2026 as a whole, down from 5% in 2025. Growth 
remains characterized by a K-shaped trajectory, and the gap between the strong performance of exports and the fragility of sectors that rely 
on domestic demand  has even widened this year . Domestic demand has weakened significantly in recent months . Retail sales have barely 
increased, the crisis in the property sector has continued and household confidence remains low. Fiscal policy measures aimed at supporting 
economic growth are expected to increase in the second half of the year. CPI inflation has rebounded slightly since last March, mostly due to 
the rise in global energy prices, as well as due to the increase in electronic good prices and anti-involution measures implemented by the 
authorities. Deflationary pressures persist, however, given weakness in domestic demand.

EUROZONE 
Eurozone growth is expected to slow to 1.1% in 2026 (from 1.3% in 2025), before strengthening to 1.5% in 2027, supported by higher defence 
and infrastructure spending as well as the global AI capex cycle. The economy has proven resilient to the energy shock, notab ly thanks to the 
increasingly tangible impact of G erman defence spending on the real economy. Elevated energy prices in the autumn (especially for natural 
gas), combined with resilient growth, are set to accelerate inflation to a peak around the turn of the year. Annual average i nflation is thus 
expected to reach  3.0% in 2026 (from 2.1% in 2025), before easing to 2.8% in 2027. Given persistent inflation and resilient growth, we 
anticipate another 25bp hike from the ECB in December 2026, bringing the deposit rate to 2.75%, followed by an extended pause . That said, 
stronger than expected second-round effects could lead to more tightening than our current baseline.

### Page 3

FRANCE 
GDP growth remained low in Q2 (0% after -0.2% in Q1) as a result of deteriorated agricultural and construction output, as well as higher 
inflation (2.4% in 2026 according to our forecast, after 0.9% in 2025) . In 2026, GDP growth should  reach 0.5% (after 0.9% in 2025), driven 
mainly by exports.

UNITED KINGDOM 
UK GDP growth would ease slightly to 1.2% in 2026 (1.3% in 2025), before edging back to 1.3% in 2027. The economy has so far weathered 
both the energy price shock and domestic political volatility. That said, growth is expected to decelerate in the second half  of the year, while 
inflation is set to remain stubbornly above target (averaging 3.2% in both 2026 and 2027), driven by higher energy prices and mounting second-
round effects. In response, we expect the BoE to deliver a single insurance hike in November 2026, bringing the Bank Rate to 4.00%. The BoE 
is then expected to resume easing in H2 2027, with two cuts taking the Bank Rate to 3.50% by end-year.

JAPAN 
Following 1.2% in 2025, Japan's GDP growth is expected to decelerate to 1.0% in 2026, before rebounding to 1.2% in 2027. Higher inflation 
and production costs associated with the energy shock are expected to weigh on the economy’s overall performance. On the other hand, the 
latter is supported by fiscal policy  and AI-related investment. Inflation has generally overshot the 2% y/y target since 2022. Accordingly, the 
Bank of Japan initiated a cautious process of “adjustment in the degree of monetary accommodation” in 2024, lifting the policy rate to 1.25% 
so far (previously negative) – the highest since 1995. We expect two more hikes by the end of Q1 2027, followed by a return to a more cautious 
pace until the terminal rate of +2.5% is reached in H2 2028. Japan is facing long-term rates pressure, illustrated by historically high 10- and 30-
year yields, probably fueled by the level of public debt and the pace of monetary adjustment.

EXCHANGE RATES 
We expect Fed hikes to temporarily offset structural headwinds and support the dollar. EUR/USD would target 1.14 by end -2026, before the 
dollar's structural downtrend resumes toward 1.20 by end-2027, although political and fiscal risk may weigh on the euro next year. The USD/JPY 
appreciation would continue but at a more moderate pace, with the BoJ still behind the curve despite its hikes. By contrast, GBP/USD would 
see relative support underpinned by a modest fiscal risk premium under the new Labour government, and resilient growth.

Document 426: “避险不再安全，仿佛回到1999年”，美银：美股防御板块泡沫已悄然超越科技股

Source PDF: all report pdf/“避险不再安全，仿佛回到1999年”，美银：美股防御板块泡沫已悄然超越科技股.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-28
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

当资⾦从 AI 交易撤出、涌向医疗和消费必需品等防御性板块时，新的泡沫⻛险正在悄然累
积。美银数据显⽰，美国医疗板块泡沫⻛险读数已升⾄约 0.8 ，消费必需品达 0.74 ，均⾼于
科技股、纳斯达克及 “ 七巨头 ” 。这⼀市场结构令⼈联想到 1999 年互联⽹泡沫顶峰，但当前
泡沫仍相对局部。随着⾦融压⼒指数升⾄ 4 ⽉以来最⾼，美银建议增加⾮对称保护，⽽⾮直
接减仓。
摘要：
当市场将医疗和消费必需品视为 AI 交易的避⻛港时，这些 " 安全资产 " 本⾝已悄然演变为新的泡
沫。美国银⾏最新研究显⽰，美股防御性板块的泡沫⻛险读数已超越科技股、纳斯达克乃⾄⽐
特币，这⼀现象与 1999 年互联⽹泡沫顶峰时期的市场结构⾼度吻合。
美银衍⽣品团队在最新⼀期《全球股票波动率洞察》报告中指出，随着 AI 交易出现动摇，资⾦涌
⼊医疗和消费必需品板块的 " 避险轮动 " 已将这两个板块的泡沫⻛险指标（ BRI ）推⾄历史⾼位。
截⾄ 10 ⽉ 23 ⽇，美国医疗板块 BRI 读数约为 0.8 ，为美银追踪的所有资产中最⾼，消费必需品约

为 0.74 ，能源约为 0.7 ；相⽐之下，科技板块仅约 0.5 ，纳斯达克约 0.3 ， " 七巨头 " 仅约 0.25 。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

这⼀格局意味着，原本⽤于对冲 AI ⻛险的 " 防御性 " 资产，其价格泡沫化程度已显著⾼于其所对冲
的标的本⾝。与此同时，美银全球⾦融压⼒指数上周从 +0.09 升⾄ +0.20 ，创 4 ⽉以来最⾼⽔平，
市场整体压⼒环境正在趋于恶化。
防御性板块 BRI 超越科技股，历史先例指向互联⽹泡沫顶峰
美银的 BRI 是⼀个纯粹基于价格的指标，综合资产的回报率、波动率、动量及 " 脆弱性 " ，⽣成 0 ⾄
1 之间的单⼀泡沫读数， 0.8 以上被标记为泡沫⻛险偏⾼。该指标曾于今年 6 ⽉率先标记纳斯达克
越过 " 泡沫阈值 " 。
美银本周新增了⼀张覆盖 30 年的 BRI 热⼒图，追踪标普 500 各板块的历史读数。数据显⽰，在
1990 年代末互联⽹狂热期间，以及 2020 年后的新冠疫情⾏情中，泡沫读数⼏乎在所有板块同步
⾛⾼，防御性板块亦不例外。
美银据此得出结论：在结构性泡沫市场中，即便基本⾯并不⽀撑，各板块均可能出现泡沫化动
态。换⾔之，当整个市场进⼊反⾝性循环，连⽛膏和制药公司也会开始像动量股⼀样交易。美

银认为，医疗和消费必需品板块出现泡沫迹象，进⼀步印证了其此前的判断 —— 市场正迈向 " ⽇
益反⾝性、易于暴涨暴跌的机制 " 。
泡沫仍属局部，与 2000 年顶峰尚有差距
尽管信号令⼈警惕，美银同时强调，当前泡沫的扩散程度远不及 2000 年。⽬前标普 500 中 BRI 超
过 0.8 的个股约为 15 只，⽽互联⽹泡沫最疯狂时期这⼀数字为 50 ⾄ 100 只。
从市值权重来看，差距更为悬殊：当前这 15 只个股合计仅占标普 500 市值的约 2.2% ，⽽互联⽹
泡沫顶峰时期这⼀⽐例⾼达 20% ⾄ 40% 。
这 15 只个股的构成颇为耐⼈寻味，⾜以令任何 1999 年的⽇内交易者感到困惑： Moderna
（ MRNA ）、 Zebra （ ZBRA ）、 CrowdStrike （ CRWD ）、 IFF 和 Revvity （ RVTY ）位居前列，可⼝
可乐（ KO ）、 Amgen （ AMGN ）、 Cboe （ CBOE ）和 Palo Alto （ PANW ）紧随其后。可⼝可乐出

现在泡沫股名单上，本⾝即是当前市场异象的最佳注脚。

### Page 3

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

泡沫蔓延⾄⾼股息与低波动主题，⾦融压⼒指数升⾄ 4 ⽉⾼位
美银已将 BRI 扩展⾄热⻔股票主题的追踪。在各主题中，云计算的泡沫读数最⾼（尽管尚未达到
极端⽔平），其次是油⽓和⾼股息。单周涨幅最⼤的则是⾼股息股票，其次是低波动率股票和⽣
物科技 —— 这意味着泡沫正在蔓延⾄那些 " 让你安⼼等待收益 " 的资产类别。
在美银⾃定义篮⼦中，泡沫热点更为分散：巴西⼤选篮⼦ BRI 读数⾼达 0.94 ，美国⽹络安全篮⼦
为 0.92 ，美国软件为 0.76 ，油⽓（不含可再⽣能源）为 0.73 ，制药超⼤规模算⼒为 0.70 ，⽇本防
御性股票同为 0.70 。读数接近于零的则是中国互联⽹、澳⻔博彩及中国反内卷篮⼦。
与此同时，美银全球⾦融压⼒指数升⾄ +0.20 ，为 4 ⽉以来最⾼，⼤宗商品和股票是压⼒最集中

的资产类别，原油隐含波动率压⼒升⾄ 7 ⽉ 31 ⽇以来最⾼，欧洲压⼒指数亦已超过⻓期中位数。
美银的 GFSI ⻛险配置器已从前⼀周的中性转为低配 4.9% 。
美银建议⾮对称保护，⽽⾮直接减仓
⾯对上述信号，美银明确表⽰， BRI ⾼读数是 " 警⽰ " 信号⽽⾮ " 离场 " 信号：历史数据显⽰，读数
超过 0.8 后，近期下⾏⻛险有所上升，但上⾏空间⼤体相当，因此该⾏倾向于增加⾮对称保护，
⽽⾮直接卖出。
美银上周推荐的具体交易策略为 " 认沽期权互换 " ：买⼊ XLP 或 XLV 的 11 ⽉认沽期权，同时卖出等
名义⾦额的 XLK 11 ⽉认沽期权，可获得⼩额净权利⾦（分别约为 0.23% 和 0.20% ，以 10 ⽉ 19 ⽇
开盘价计算）。
这⼀策略的逻辑在于：当市场中所有⼈都将资⾦藏⼊同⼀批 " 安全 " 标的时，这些标的便不再安
全。互联⽹泡沫时期的热⼒图提醒投资者，当流动性漫灌整个市场，泡沫最终会出现在每⼀个
⻆落，包括那些本应免疫的防御性板块。随着⾦融压⼒升⾄ 4 ⽉⾼位，下⼀阶段的市场焦点，或

许将从 " 持有哪个泡沫 " 转向 " 哪个泡沫先破裂 " 。

Document 442: 【國泰證期研究部】衛星產業-SpaceX D2C進展更進一步，星艦試射進展順利-20261016

Source PDF: all report pdf/【國泰證期研究部】衛星產業-SpaceX D2C進展更進一步，星艦試射進展順利-20261016.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-16
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

1 
產業更新 – 衛星產業 
SpaceX D2C 進展更進一步，星艦試射進展順利 
What’s New –  SpaceX 收購新頻譜，劍指行動通訊市場 
SpaceX於 10/8宣布收購 Grain Management旗下全美 800MHz低頻頻譜，涵蓋最多 14MHz
配對頻譜，交易金額估約 80 億美元。 800MHz 具備廣域覆蓋及室內穿透優勢，可與既有
2GHz頻譜互補，改善偏遠地區通訊品質。儘管仍待 FCC核准，惟主席 Brendan Carr 對新業
者投資持正面態度，預期有利交易推進。 
Trends/Outlook – 衛星、火箭升級短期影響衛星部屬速度 
3Q26 SpaceX共完成 22次 Starlink低軌衛星部署任務，合計發射 578顆衛星，季減 16.8%，
9M26累計部署 2,167顆。單季發射數量下滑主要受任務頻率減少影響，惟單次平均部署 26.3
顆，略高於 2Q26的 25.7顆。此外，SpaceX於 9月成功透過 Starship部署 26顆新一代 V3
衛星，顯示大型運載火箭逐步具備實際衛星部署能力。另一方面，Amazon Leo於 3Q26僅完
成 1次發射任務，部署 29顆衛星，季減 81.3%，主要受運載火箭供應及發射排程限制，後續
須觀察 Vulcan、Ariane 6 及 New Glenn 等新一代火箭投入商業發射的進度。SpaceX另規劃
於路易斯安那州興建全球第四座太空港，預計 2027年動工、2029年首飛，規劃設置逾 12座
發射塔，全面建成後目標每年支援數千次 Starship飛行任務。

Investment Summary 
1. 研究部認為，EchoStar交易解決了 Starlink下一代 D2C的頻譜與容量問題，而近期收購
Grain Management 旗下全美 800MHz低頻頻譜，則進一步補強低頻地面覆蓋資源。有
了自有頻譜資源，搭配未來新一代 D2C衛星酬載之升級，SpaceX 已逐步豐富期從衛星寬
頻業者，走向整合型行動通訊服務供應商的完整資源。 
2. 維持 SpaceX 26/27 年新增 4,000/6,000 顆衛星預期不變，儘管 3Q26 新增數略低於預期，
短期受到新世代衛星、火箭轉換，影響現階段 Falcon 9星鏈任務次數部屬，惟考量下一代 
V3 衛星試射順利，4Q26有望啟動部屬，單次 Starlink 低軌衛星發射數量上限將由 29 顆
大幅上升至  60 顆，將有益於未來衛星成長在加速 ，故持續看好天上衛星供應鏈如華通
(2313 TT)、昇達科(3491 TT)、燿華(2367 TT)後市發展。

2 
SpaceX 收購新頻譜，劍指行動通訊市場 
SpaceX於 10/8宣布與數位基礎建設投資公司 Grain Management達成協議，擬收購美國全國性
800MHz低頻頻譜牌照組合，涵蓋最多 14MHz配對頻譜，市場預估交易金額約 80億美元。由於
800MHz具備較佳的訊號穿透能力及廣域覆蓋優勢，可與既有 2GHz中頻頻譜形成互補，提升室內
及偏遠地區通訊品質。該收購案目前雖仍需要 FCC進行批准，不過 FCC主席 Brendan Carr目前已
表態，認為更多投資與服務業者加入，將有助於推動新一代連線技術發展，因此預期此次收購將順
利完成，未來將加速 Starlink Mobile整合衛星與地面行動網路，強化 D2D服務覆蓋能力，並逐步
降低對傳統電信商的依賴，為 SpaceX進軍美國行動通訊市場奠定基礎。 
SpaceX於 25年進行第一次的頻譜收購，向美國衛星及電信業者 EchoStar收購旗下無線通訊頻譜
使用權及相關資產，主要獲得 AWS-4 使用權之 2GHz頻譜，初步奠定了 SpaceX在行動衛星通訊
的基礎，且逐步掌握自主頻譜資源。然衛星通訊連線存在的痛點之一，便是經常因建築物、遮蔽物
等造成訊號傳遞出現障礙，因此過去 Starlink用戶終端天線皆需要安裝在水平面 15 度以上、天空
完全無遮蔽的屋頂或陽台上。而在新取得的低頻頻譜後，SpaceX 將具備傳輸距離遠、可穿透建築
物及樹木等障礙物的優勢，有助改善衛星行動通訊的室內訊號覆蓋能力，降低對傳統基地台的依賴。
另外目前大多數行動手機均支援 800MHz頻段，因此未來消費者若使用 Starlink Mobile服務，無
須另外購買專用衛星電話。 
圖一：Starlink Mobile 服務應用示意圖                                               圖二：D2C 衛星酬載 
資料來源：SpaceX、國泰證期研究部整理 
研究部認為，EchoStar交易解決了 Starlink下一代 D2C的頻譜與容量問題，而近期的收購則進一
步補強低頻地面覆蓋資源。有了自有頻譜資源，搭配未來新一代 D2C衛星酬載之升級，SpaceX 已
逐步豐富期從衛星寬頻業者，走向整合型行動通訊服務供應商的完整資源。

### Page 3

投資 顧問 
產業報告 
 2026/10/16

3 
新世代衛星、火箭升級短期影響衛星部屬速度 
3Q26 SpaceX共計完成 22次 Starlink低軌衛星部署任務，合計發射 598顆衛星，包含次軌道測試
衛星 20顆，季減 11.1%，年初累計至 9M26共部署 2,167顆。其中 7、8、9月分別部署 210、
261及 107顆，9月發射數量明顯下降，推測因 Starship試飛任務導致 Falcon 9 星鏈任務減少影
響，單季任務數由 2Q26的 27次降至 22次。然 3Q26單次任務平均部署衛星顆數約為 26.3顆，
略高於 2Q26的 25.7顆，顯示整體發射數量下滑主要來自任務頻率減少，而非單次載運數量下降。
此外，SpaceX於 9月 28日成功透過 Starship Flight 14 將 26顆新一代 Starlink V3 衛星送入軌
道，為 Starship首次完成正式衛星入軌部署。隨著下一代衛星導入更大型天線與高功率通訊酬載，
單顆衛星重量及通訊容量同步提升，目前短期可能限制 Falcon 9單次部署數量，但長期隨 Starship
發射能力逐步成熟，有望加速高容量 Starlink V3衛星部署，帶動整體星系寬頻容量持續擴張。 
此外，另一位衛星運營商 Amazon Leo 3Q26僅完成 1次低軌衛星發射任務，部署 29顆衛星，數
量大幅季減 81.3%，年初至今共部署 216顆衛星。3Q26發射任務於 7/2由 ULA Atlas V 火箭執
行，其後 8、9月均無新增衛星部署，顯示 Amazon Leo 衛星部署進度仍受到運載火箭供應及發射
排程限制。我們認為，Amazon Leo目前主要瓶頸在於發射運能，而非衛星製造能力，後續須觀察
包含 ULA Vulcan、Arianespace Ariane 6及 Blue Origin New Glenn 等新一代火箭逐步投入商業
發射，是否能夠帶動 Amazon Leo發射進程提升。 
圖三：Starbase Louisiana 示意圖 
資料來源：interstellar gateway、國泰證期研究部整理 
SpaceX 於今年 8 月拍板定案，將於美國路易斯安那州南部 Vermilion Parish興建全球第四座太空
發射基地（Starbase Louisiana） ，基地位於墨西哥灣沿岸，占地約12.5萬英畝（約 5.1萬公頃） ，
面積超過紐約曼哈頓 8倍，預計 2027年正式動工，並於 2029年執行首次 Starship發射任務。基
地規劃設置超過 12座發射塔，全面建成後目標每年支援數千次 Starship飛行任務。研究部預期，
隨著 Starship逐步實現高頻率發射與火箭重複使用，新增發射基地將有助於突破既有太空港的發射
排程及運能限制，進一步加速 Starlink下一代 V3衛星部署，並提升 SpaceX大規模建置低軌衛星
的能力。

非核心消費產業投資攻略
出 刊 日 : 2026/10/13

• 非核心消費板塊獲利前景近
期蒙上陰霾。首先，郵輪業
雖受惠於旅遊需求韌性而上
調 2026 財年預測，但高利
率與高油價恐使後市持續承
壓；其次，運動鞋服產業受
到大中華區市場衰退與新興
小品牌崛起雙重打擊，面臨
營收與獲利雙雙下滑的困境
，短期內組織重整效益恐難
顯現。再者，在即將迎來的
財報季前夕，市場共識普遍
預期 3Q26 的增長將陷入停
滯。
《市場共識預期3Q26非核心消費板塊成長停滯》
美3Q26財報季將陸續揭曉 ，目前市場共識預期標
普500指數各產業板塊第三季的獲利成長存在較大
差異，其中資訊科技和能源板塊將推動指數整體獲
利成長，預期非核心消費板塊整體每股盈餘將不會
成長。另外，零星個股捎來截至 8月底的季度業績
也顯示好壞參半的結果。
《郵輪旅遊需求仍具韌性，燃油成本仍是潛在風險》
嘉年華郵輪 (CCL)3Q26財年(截至8月底)營收年增
3.5%，每股盈餘持平 ，淨收益率超預期 ，主要貢
獻來自船上及其它收入，然因燃料成本大增36%，
完全抵銷了營運效率與成本控制的效益 ，導致調整

後EBITDA持平。管理層上調2026年全年獲利展望 ，
且公司表示 2027年訂位量及訂位價格均創下歷史
新高，目前已售出約半數 2027年艙位 ，顯示旅遊
需求仍具韌性。但市場對其未來半年的獲利預期偏
共識預期整體非核心消費3Q26盈餘0成長

保守，主要是9 月以來居高不下的利率與一路攀升

《運動鞋服Nike轉型成效慢，獲利展望不佳》
Nike1Q27財年(2026/6~2026/8)營收與每股盈餘
皆衰退。運動休閒系列、Jordan品牌和大中華區市
場下滑 ，成為主要逆風 。管理層預期 2027財年營
收將出現高個位數下滑，提出的每股盈餘財測也低
於市場預期 。即便Nike推出名為 Pace營運重整計
畫，但效益明顯無法在短期內看到 。其中一個原因
是Nike的市場主導地位已不如前，小型品牌如今已

具備規模化營運能力，當消費者行為已發生結構性
轉變時(品牌忠誠度降低與多品牌嘗試 )，如何收復
市佔率就成為一項艱巨且持久的挑戰。
《2026 美年底消費總額預期創新高 ，逾半增長靠
「通膨」，電商與生成式AI成雙引擎》
近期已有多家研調機構針對美國2026年年底消費
旺季出具預測報告。整體來看，各大機構預測的銷
售總額皆創下歷史新高 ，但其背後的共同隱憂是
「通膨」，直言超過一半的名目增長率是由物價上
漲驅動。這使得消費者普遍展現「價格敏感、主動
尋找折扣、切換品牌以省錢」的價值導向行為。電
商增速(網購)依然大幅碾壓實體零售。所有機構也
都點出生成式AI在2026年成為主流購物工具。
資料來源：Mastercard， 2026/9/23，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10/13

機構預測2026年底消費旺季
各家機構重點分述如下
Mastercard ： 預 期 假日消費成長近半因物價上漲 ，
今年主因能源與AI建設成本增加，去年則受關稅影
響。鑑於柴油價格持續高企可能推高零售商的運輸
成本，並對年底的整體消費價格構成上行壓力，因
此值得密切關注。高收入家庭因財富成長、增加自
由支配支出，成為近年消費成長的重要引領者， 然
而，受益於勞動市場穩定、薪資成長與健康資產負
債表，各收入群體亦持續穩定貢獻，消費仍由廣大
家庭共同支撐。
Adobe：預計今年假期季節將推出極具競爭力的折

扣，雖然消費者歷來都熱衷於利用節日促銷活動購
買禮物或添加最新電子產品，但許多人現在更傾向
於利用折扣囤積生活必需品。另，今年假期季節美
國零售網站的人工智慧流 量 將年增130%。預計感
恩節的增幅最大(年增159%)，其次是黑色星期五(
年成長 95%)和網路星期一(年增88%)。
德勤(Deloitte) ： 消費者仍然重視為親朋好友營造
特別的節日氛圍，同時也在精打細算地消費。為了
更好地利用每一分錢，觀察到各個收入階層的消費
者都在追求性價比，例如在不同品牌和零售商之間
切換，以及利用促銷活動來控制支出。

3Q26 財報前瞻：AWS 持續加速成長，惟買方投資人預期已堆高 
重要訊息  
Amazo n 預計於台灣時間 10/30 凌晨公 布 3Q26 財報。 
評論及分析  
Holiday  Season 線上銷售 年增率 預期持平。根據 Adobe  Analytics 預估美
國 2026 購物旺季(11 -12 月)線上銷售額將達 US$275bn 年增 6.7% vs. 2025
年增 6.8% 大致持平，係來自於全品項折扣幅度約 18-30%與 2025 相當，AI
連結導購至網站年增 130% 。4M26 起徵收 3.5% 燃油及物流附加費，禁止
Meta Muse 或未與公司充分 溝通的 Agent 服務取得 網站資料，Amazon Ads
與 ChatGPT 試行對話式廣告投放，皆為應對高油價及 AI 變革之措施。我們
正向看待電商業務對 3Q26 財報/4Q26 財測為正向助益 。 
AWS 持續加速 成長 ，惟 買方投資人 預期已堆高 。 根據 ： (1) 媒體揭露
Anthropic  2Q/3Q26 營收 US$11.6/16.8bn ，我們以推論 +訓練算力及通路費
用加總佔比營收 40-50%計算，AWS 為 Anthropic 主要算力供應商及銷售渠
道(佔比預估 60%)； (2)GPT -6 獲得正向回饋並已 在 Bedrock 上架，OpenAI
與 AWS 合作逐漸緊密，惟 2GW Trn 採用將於 2027 上線；(3)標準化算力價
格 持續回升尤其在 Blackwell 系 列更為明顯， EC2 部分服務於 7/1 調漲
20% ，10/7 將再調漲 15%；(4)管理層股東信指出 AWS 若直接外售 自研晶
片或硬體系統將增加 US$30bn 潛在營收。 我們預期 AWS 營收年增率於
2H26-2027 持續加速成長並優於賣方市場共識，惟我們觀察到買方投資人對
2H26 成長預期已達 40%+ 與賣方市場共識出現落差。  
資本支出 同時支 應 AI Labs  / Enterprise  / Rufus 成長 。管理層先前指出
4Q25/2025 新增算力 1.2/3.9GW ，2027 年底算力容量翻倍，我們估算
2027 AWS Trn/NVDA VR 每 GW 建置成本 US$25- 30bn/US$55 -60bn ，AWS
每 GW 平均建置成本約 US$40-45bn 下緣，AWS 資本支出佔總資本支出
90%(其於為物流基礎設施 )，我們預期 2027 資本支出 US$300-330bn，故
市場共識將再上修約 11%，對 FCF 而言為龐大負擔。  
投資建議  
我們維持 Amazon 「增加持股」建議 並調升 目標價 340 美元，係以 2027 年
GAAP 每股盈餘 32 倍換算 。短期 Muse 雜音/ 回檔將創造長線布局良機。  
投資風險  
市場競爭；反壟斷議題 ；企業 IT 支出或消費動能 趨緩。

### Page 6

美國 Amazon

十月 20, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 6

項目 定義 
能源使用 
直接與間接能源消耗總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司運營範圍內消耗之能源總量 
- 能源使用總量 = 直接能源消耗總量 + 間接能源消耗量 
- 總能源使用量包括購買的能源、生產的能源 
- 就公用事業而言，輸電/電網損耗為其業務活動之一部分，被視為總能源消耗，數據不包括為滿足能源使用而生產的電力（公用
事業為出售而生產） 
- 就公用事業而言，用於能源生產的煤、天然氣或核能等原料不屬於「總能源使用」項下 
購買之再生能源 
Primary 再生能源購買總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司各種來源之能源消耗量與購買的能源中屬於自然界可再生者（太陽能、風能、水能、生物質能、地熱能）之量  
- 如無證據顯示再生能源由公司生產，則所報告的能源數據視為購買的再生能源 
再生能源使用率 再生能源占總能源消耗量之比例 
CO2 約當排放量 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量(公噸) 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍 1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳 (CO2)、甲烷 (CH4)、一氧化二氮 (N2O)、氫氟碳化物 (HFCS)、全氟化合物 (PFCS)、六氟化硫 (SF6)、三氟化氮 
(NF3) 
CO2 約當排放量營收比 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量（公噸）銷售（百萬元）比 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍 1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳 (CO2)、甲烷 (CH4)、一氧化二氮 (N2O)、氫氟碳化物 (HFCS)、全氟化合物 (PFCS)、六氟化硫 (SF6)、三氟化氮 
(NF3) 
廢棄物總量 
廢棄物總量(公噸) 
- 總廢棄物 = 無害廢棄物 + 有害廢棄物 
- 僅考慮固體廢棄物，但如液體廢棄物以公噸為單位呈報，則會將其加入求得包含液體廢棄物之總量 
- 對於採礦、石油與天然氣等行業，tailings、廢石、煤、飛灰等廢棄物亦考慮在內 
廢棄物回收率 
公司呈報的廢棄物回收率 
- 廢棄物回收率 = 廢棄物回收量/總廢棄物*100 
- 廢棄物轉化為能源或經由廢棄物焚燒產生能源視為廢棄物回收 
- 經由堆肥回收的廢棄物視為回收之廢棄物 
總取水量 
總取水量(立方公尺) 
- 由呈報組織直接或經水公司等中介機構從任何水源抽取的總水量 
- 水井、城鎮/公用事業/市政用水、河水、地表水等不同之水源均予以考慮 
環保支出 環保支出總金額 - 所有用於環境保護，或防止、減少、控制環境因素、影響、危害的投資與支出，亦包括處置、處理、衛生、清理支出 
員工流動率 
員工流動率 
- 包括任何原因（自願或非自願）離開公司的員工，如辭職、退休、自然離職/死亡、醫療失能、冗員、裁員、重組、解僱、裁減
或定期合約到期 
- 員工流動率 =（離開之員工/平均員工人數）*100 
- 平均員工人數 =（本年末員工人數 + 去年末員工人數）/2 
- 本財年末員工數 =上個財年末員工數 + 新員工數 - 離開之員工數 
女性經理 
女性經理百分比 
- 女性經理占公司經理的百分比 
- 如有不同階層之百分比，如最高階、高階、中階、初階，則會考慮中階女性經理之百分比 
- 女性經理百分比 = 女性經理人數/經理總數*100 
女性員工 
女性員工百分比 
- 女性員工占公司員工總數的百分比 
- 女性員工百分比 = 女性人數/員工總數*100 
教育訓練總時數 
所有員工教育訓練總時數 
- 僅考慮員工教育訓練時數 
- 包括一般員工所有類型的教育訓練（如健康與安全、環境、急難事件救援、技能與職業發展） 
- 如果數據以天為單位，則乘以 8，係假設 1 天 =  8 小時工作 
每位員工教育訓練時數 平均每年每位員工總教育訓練時數 
股東治理分數 衡量公司用以反收購工具的有效性 
公司治理分數 衡量公司對最佳治理原則的承諾和有效性 
產品責任分數 衡量公司生產優質產品和服務的能力，且產品是否將客戶的健康、安全、整合性和數據隱私進行綜合考量 
社區關係分數 衡量公司對成為優良公民、保護公眾健康和尊重商業道德的承諾 
勞動力分數 衡量公司在員工工作滿意度、健康、工作場所的安全、多樣性、平等以及員工發展機會方面的成效 
資源使用指標 衡量公司在原物料、能源或水的使用效率，以及是否通過改進供應鏈來尋求更俱生態效率的解決方案
